--
-- PM  (User) 
--
CREATE USER PM
  IDENTIFIED BY <password>
  DEFAULT TABLESPACE USERS
  TEMPORARY TABLESPACE TEMP
  PROFILE DEFAULT
  ACCOUNT UNLOCK;

-- 2 Roles for PM 
--
-- CONNECT  (Role) 
--
GRANT CONNECT TO PM;
--
-- DBA  (Role) 
--
GRANT DBA TO PM;
ALTER USER PM DEFAULT ROLE ALL;

-- 1 System Privilege for PM 
GRANT UNLIMITED TABLESPACE TO PM;


--
-- AUDIT_AUDITOR  (Table) 
--
--   Row Count: 32
CREATE TABLE PM.AUDIT_AUDITOR
(
  AUDITOR_ID        VARCHAR2(30 CHAR)           NOT NULL,
  AUDITOR_NAME      VARCHAR2(100 CHAR)          NOT NULL,
  AUDITOR_STAFF_ID  VARCHAR2(30 CHAR),
  AUDITOR_MOBILE    VARCHAR2(15 CHAR),
  AUDITOR_EMAIL     VARCHAR2(20 CHAR),
  ACT               NUMBER(1)                   DEFAULT 1,
  CDT               DATE                        DEFAULT TO_DATE(SYSDATE),
  CDU               VARCHAR2(10 CHAR),
  UDT               DATE                        DEFAULT TO_DATE(SYSDATE),
  UDU               VARCHAR2(10 CHAR)
)
TABLESPACE USERS
PCTUSED    0
PCTFREE    10
INITRANS   1
MAXTRANS   255
STORAGE    (
            INITIAL          64K
            NEXT             1M
            MINEXTENTS       1
            MAXEXTENTS       UNLIMITED
            PCTINCREASE      0
            BUFFER_POOL      DEFAULT
           );


--
-- AUDIT_AUDITOR_NAME_PK  (Index) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_AUDITOR (Table)
--
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX PM.AUDIT_AUDITOR_NAME_PK ON PM.AUDIT_AUDITOR
(AUDITOR_ID)
TABLESPACE USERS
PCTFREE    10
INITRANS   2
MAXTRANS   255
STORAGE    (
            INITIAL          64K
            NEXT             1M
            MINEXTENTS       1
            MAXEXTENTS       UNLIMITED
            PCTINCREASE      0
            BUFFER_POOL      DEFAULT
           );

ALTER TABLE PM.AUDIT_AUDITOR ADD (
  CONSTRAINT AUDIT_AUDITOR_NAME_PK
  PRIMARY KEY
  (AUDITOR_ID)
  USING INDEX PM.AUDIT_AUDITOR_NAME_PK
  ENABLE VALIDATE);

--
-- INSERT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL_DATA  (Procedure) 
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.INSERT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL_DATA AS

BEGIN
   -- Loop through the data from VW_LIC_EXPIRE_NOTIFY_MAIL
    FOR rec IN (
        SELECT MAIL_TO, MAIL_CC, MAIL_BCC, MAIL_SUBJECT, MAIL_BODY, REFNO
        FROM VW_ADT_FST_FLW_UP_DAY_BEFORE
    ) LOOP
        -- Insert data into T_MAILOUT@Mail_db_link
        INSERT INTO T_MAILOUT@Mail_db_link (
            MAIL_TO, MAIL_CC, MAIL_BCC, MAIL_SUBJT, MAIL_BODYHTML, MAIL_REFERENCE
        )
        VALUES (
            rec.MAIL_TO,
            'ga@prangroup.com;mis@prangroup.com;mis4@prangroup.com;mis29@mis.prangroup.com;mis34@mis.prangroup.com',
            rec.MAIL_BCC,
            rec.MAIL_SUBJECT,
            rec.MAIL_BODY,  -- Assuming MAIL_BODY goes to MAIL_BODYHTML
            rec.REFNO   -- Assuming TRN_DATE goes to MAIL_SENDTIME
        );
    END LOOP;

    -- Commit the transaction to save changes
    COMMIT;

    -- Output success message
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Data successfully inserted into T_MAILOUT@Mail_db_link.');
EXCEPTION
    WHEN OTHERS THEN
        -- In case of an error, roll back the transaction
        ROLLBACK;
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Error: ' || SQLERRM);
END insert_license_mail_data;
/


--
-- PRO_ADT_COM_GROUP_WISE_RPT  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_REPORT (Table)
--   AUDIT_TASK (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   C_COMPANY_GROUP (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_ADT_COM_GROUP_WISE_RPT (USERID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
/* Company Group Wise Total Report Data Generator*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_D NUMBER(10);
VALUE_E NUMBER(10);
VALUE_T NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT  NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  AS TOTAL_REPORT  INTO VALUE_A FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
                LEFT JOIN 
                    AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
                INNER JOIN 
                    C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
                WHERE 
                    R.ACT = 1 AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID=1;

SELECT  NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  AS TOTAL_REPORT  INTO VALUE_B FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
                LEFT JOIN 
                    AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
                INNER JOIN 
                    C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
                WHERE 
                    R.ACT = 1 AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID=2;
 
SELECT  NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  AS TOTAL_REPORT  INTO VALUE_C FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
                LEFT JOIN 
                    AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
                INNER JOIN 
                    C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
                WHERE 
                    R.ACT = 1 AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID=3;

SELECT  NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  AS TOTAL_REPORT  INTO VALUE_D FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
                LEFT JOIN 
                    AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
                INNER JOIN 
                    C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
                WHERE 
                    R.ACT = 1 AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID=5;

SELECT  NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  AS TOTAL_REPORT  INTO VALUE_E FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
                LEFT JOIN 
                    AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
                INNER JOIN 
                    C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
                WHERE 
                    R.ACT = 1 AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID=4;
                 
SELECT  NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  AS TOTAL_REPORT INTO VALUE_T  FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
                LEFT JOIN 
                    AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
                INNER JOIN 
                    C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
                WHERE 
                    R.ACT = 1;
                    
UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C, DATA_D=VALUE_D, DATA_E=VALUE_E, DATA_J=VALUE_T,
UDU=USERID,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE)
WHERE SV_TABLE='COMPAY_GROUP_WISE_REPORT_SUM';
COMMIT;
END;
/


--
-- PRO_ADT_CURRENT_LAST_MON_RPT  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_REPORT (Table)
--   AUDIT_TASK (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   C_COMPANY_GROUP (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_ADT_CURRENT_LAST_MON_RPT(USERID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
/* Current Last Month Report Sum Data Generator*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_D NUMBER(10);
VALUE_E NUMBER(10);
VALUE_F NUMBER(10);
VALUE_G NUMBER(10);
VALUE_H NUMBER(10);
BEGIN

SELECT NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0) AS THIS_MONTH_TOTAL_REPORT INTO VALUE_A 
            FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
            LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
            INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
            WHERE R.ACT = 1 
AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 1 AND TRUNC(R.CDT) BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM') AND TRUNC(LAST_DAY(SYSDATE)); 

SELECT NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0) INTO VALUE_B 
            FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
            LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
            INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
            WHERE R.ACT = 1 
AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 2 AND TRUNC(R.CDT) BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM') AND TRUNC(LAST_DAY(SYSDATE)); 

SELECT NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0) INTO VALUE_C 
            FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
            LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
            INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
            WHERE R.ACT = 1 
AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 3 AND TRUNC(R.CDT) BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM') AND TRUNC(LAST_DAY(SYSDATE)); 

SELECT NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0) INTO VALUE_D 
            FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
            LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
            INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
            WHERE R.ACT = 1 
AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 5 AND TRUNC(R.CDT) BETWEEN TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM') AND TRUNC(LAST_DAY(SYSDATE)); 



SELECT NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  INTO VALUE_E
        FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
        LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
        INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
        WHERE R.ACT = 1
AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 1 AND TRUNC(R.CDT) BETWEEN TRUNC(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -1), 'MM') AND LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -1));

SELECT NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  INTO VALUE_F
        FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
        LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
        INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
        WHERE R.ACT = 1
AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 2 AND TRUNC(R.CDT) BETWEEN TRUNC(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -1), 'MM') AND LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -1));

SELECT NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  INTO VALUE_G
        FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
        LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
        INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
        WHERE R.ACT = 1
AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 3 AND TRUNC(R.CDT) BETWEEN TRUNC(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -1), 'MM') AND LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -1));

SELECT NVL(COUNT(R.AUDIT_RPT_ID), 0)  INTO VALUE_H
        FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
        LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
        INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
        WHERE R.ACT = 1
AND CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 5 AND TRUNC(R.CDT) BETWEEN TRUNC(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -1), 'MM') AND LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, -1));

UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C, DATA_D=VALUE_D,
DATA_E=VALUE_E, DATA_F=VALUE_F, DATA_G=VALUE_G, DATA_H=VALUE_H,
UDU=USERID,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE)
WHERE SV_TABLE='CURRENT_LAST_MONTH_REPORT_SUM';
COMMIT;
END;
/


--
-- PRO_ADT_REPORT_SENT_STATUS  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_REPORT (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_ADT_REPORT_SENT_STATUS
IS

VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_T NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
select COUNT(AUDIT_RPT_ID) INTO VALUE_A from AUDIT_REPORT WHERE ACT=1 AND RPT_SENT_DATE IS NOT NULL;
select COUNT(AUDIT_RPT_ID) INTO VALUE_B from AUDIT_REPORT WHERE ACT=1 AND RPT_SENT_DATE IS  NULL;
select COUNT(AUDIT_RPT_ID) INTO VALUE_T  from AUDIT_REPORT WHERE ACT=1;
UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B,DATA_J=VALUE_T,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE)
WHERE SV_TABLE='AUDIT_REPORT_SENT_SUM';
COMMIT;
END;
/


--
-- PRO_ADT_REPORT_STATUS  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_TASK (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_ADT_REPORT_STATUS
IS
/*Asset Data Summery Generator -KSA*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_T NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_A FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE TASK_STATUS=5 AND REPORT_CREATE_STATUS=1 AND REPORT_STATUS_ID=1  AND ACT=1;
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_B FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE TASK_STATUS=5 AND REPORT_CREATE_STATUS=0 AND REPORT_STATUS_ID=2  AND ACT=1;
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_C FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE TASK_STATUS=5 AND REPORT_CREATE_STATUS=0 AND REPORT_STATUS_ID=1  AND ACT=1;
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_T FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE TASK_STATUS=5 AND ACT=1;

UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C, DATA_J=VALUE_T,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE) 
WHERE SV_TABLE='AUDIT_REPORT_STATUS';
COMMIT;
END;
/


--
-- PRO_ADT_TASK_STATUS  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_TASK (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_ADT_TASK_STATUS
IS
/*Asset Data Summery Generator -KSA*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_D NUMBER(10);
VALUE_E NUMBER(10);
VALUE_T NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_A FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE TASK_STATUS=1 AND ACT=1;
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_B FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE TASK_STATUS=2 AND ACT=1;
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_C FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE TASK_STATUS=3 AND ACT=1;
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_D FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE TASK_STATUS=4 AND ACT=1;
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_E FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE TASK_STATUS=5 AND ACT=1;
SELECT COUNT (TASK_ID) INTO VALUE_T FROM AUDIT_TASK WHERE ACT=1;

UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C, DATA_D=VALUE_D, DATA_E=VALUE_E, DATA_J=VALUE_T,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE) 
WHERE SV_TABLE='AUDIT_TASK_STATUS_DATA';
COMMIT;
END;
/


--
-- PRO_DAMAGE_LOST_OWNERSHIP  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_DAMAGE_LOST_OWNERSHIP (ASSETOID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
--ASSET DAMAGE, OWNERSHIP, LOST, SALES,
--IBNE KAYESH
--23-DEC-2019
REG_DATE DATE;
ASET_PRICE    NUMBER (10);
BEGIN
SELECT A.REG_DATE, A.ASET_PRIC INTO REG_DATE, ASET_PRICE FROM PM.P_ASSET A WHERE A.ASET_OID=ASSETOID;
UPDATE PM.P_ASSET_DAMAGE X SET X.DMG_SRV_DURA=TO_DATE(X.DMG_DATE, 'dd-mm-yy')  - TO_DATE(REG_DATE, 'dd-mm-yy'), X.DMG_PRIC_DIFF=ASET_PRICE - X.DMG_PRICE WHERE X.ASET_OID=ASSETOID;
COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_DAMAGE_LOST_OWNERSHIP_APP  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_DAMAGE_LOST_OWNERSHIP_APP (ASSETOID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
--ASSET DAMAGE, OWNERSHIP, LOST, SALES, Approval
--IBNE KAYESH
--23-DEC-2019
DMG_TYPE       VARCHAR2(2 BYTE);
BEGIN
SELECT  D.DMG_TYPE INTO DMG_TYPE FROM PM.P_ASSET_DAMAGE  D WHERE D.ASET_OID=ASSETOID;
UPDATE  PM.P_ASSET_DAMAGE X SET X.DMG_ACTV=1 WHERE X.ASET_OID=ASSETOID;
COMMIT;
UPDATE  PM.P_ASSET A SET A.ASET_CLSE=DMG_TYPE,A.ASET_ACTV=0 WHERE A.ASET_OID=ASSETOID;
COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_EXPD_XL  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_EXPD_XL (ASSETOID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
VALUE_ONE    NUMBER (10);
VALUE_TWO    VARCHAR2 (30 CHAR);
OWNR_COUNT NUMBER;
-----------------------------------10-Dec-2019 Re-write--------------------------------
BEGIN
VALUE_ONE := 0;
VALUE_TWO := 0;
SELECT COUNT(OWNR_TEXT) INTO OWNR_COUNT FROM PM.P_OWNR WHERE OWNR_ACTV=1 AND OWNR_ASET=ASSETOID;
IF OWNR_COUNT =1 THEN 
---------------------------GET User OID--------------------------------
SELECT OWNR_OID INTO VALUE_TWO FROM PM.P_OWNR WHERE OWNR_ACTV=1 AND OWNR_ASET=ASSETOID;
---------------------------GET LAST OID--------------------------------
SELECT TO_NUMBER(SEQ_NUMBER) + 1 INTO VALUE_ONE FROM PM.P_SEQ WHERE SEQ_TEXT = '30';
--------------------------- INSERT FINAL STATEMENT--------------------------------
INSERT INTO PM.P_EXPD (EXPD_TEXT,EXPD_EXPH,EXPD_ASET,EXPD_OWNR,EXPD_AMNT,EXPD_DATE,EXPD_AUTH,EXPD_NOTE)
SELECT  VALUE_ONE,'10000',ASET_OID,VALUE_TWO,EXPD_AMNT,EXPD_DATE,0,EXPD_NOTE FROM PM.P_EXPD_XL WHERE ASET_OID=ASSETOID;
COMMIT;
----------------------------------After Insert Delete from XL-------------------------------------
DELETE FROM PM.P_EXPD_XL  WHERE ASET_OID=ASSETOID;
COMMIT;
----------------------------------UPDATE OID-------------------------------------
UPDATE PM.P_SEQ SET SEQ_NUMBER=TO_NUMBER(SEQ_NUMBER) + 1 WHERE SEQ_TEXT = '30';
COMMIT;
ELSE
----------------------------------No user found Delete from XL-------------------------------------
DELETE FROM PM.P_EXPD_XL  WHERE ASET_OID=ASSETOID;
COMMIT;
END IF;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_EXPD_XL_JUNK_CLEAR  (Procedure) 
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_EXPD_XL_JUNK_CLEAR
AS
BEGIN
----09-Dec-2019
DELETE FROM PM.P_EXPD_XL X
      WHERE X.ASET_OID IN (SELECT A.ASET_OID
                             FROM PM.P_ASSET A
                            WHERE A.ISBOOKED = 0
                            AND A.ASET_ACTV=1);
                            COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_INSC_XL  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_INSC_XL (ASSETOID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
VALUE_ONE    NUMBER (10);
VALUE_TWO    VARCHAR2 (30 CHAR);
ASET_COUNT NUMBER;
OWNR_COUNT NUMBER;
BEGIN
VALUE_ONE := 0;
VALUE_TWO := 0;
-------------------Find Active ASET--------------------------
SELECT COUNT(ASET_TEXT) AS ASET_COUN INTO ASET_COUNT FROM PM.P_ASSET WHERE ASET_ACTV=1 AND ASET_CLSE='N' AND ASET_OID=ASSETOID;
IF ASET_COUNT =1 THEN
-------------------Find Active User If Exist Else Set Default--------------------------
    SELECT COUNT(OWNR_TEXT) AS OWNR_COUN INTO OWNR_COUNT FROM PM.P_OWNR WHERE OWNR_ACTV=1 AND OWNR_ASET=ASSETOID;
        IF OWNR_COUNT > 0 THEN
            SELECT OWNR_OID INTO VALUE_TWO FROM PM.P_OWNR WHERE OWNR_ACTV=1 AND OWNR_ASET=ASSETOID;
        END IF;
---------------------------INSC_TEXT get last OID--------------------------------
SELECT TO_NUMBER(SEQ_NUMBER) + 1 INTO VALUE_ONE FROM PM.P_SEQ WHERE SEQ_TEXT = '40';
---------------------------INSC_TEXT Insert Final Statement--------------------------------
INSERT INTO PM.P_INSC (INSC_TEXT,INSC_EXPH,INSC_ASET,INSC_USER,INSC_PLCY,INSC_RDAT,INSC_PREM,INSC_AMNT,INSC_EDAT,INSC_NOTE,INSC_ACTV)
SELECT VALUE_ONE,'10001',INSC_ASET,VALUE_TWO,INSC_PLCY,INSC_RDAT,INSC_PREM, ROUND(INSC_PREM+(INSC_PREM*0.15))-ROUND(INSC_PREM *0.8) AS INSC_AMNT,TO_DATE(SYSDATE,'DD/MM/YY') AS INSC_EDAT,INSC_NOTE,1 FROM PM.P_INSC_XL WHERE INSC_ASET=ASSETOID;
COMMIT;
----------------------------------Delete from Temp Table-------------------------------------
DELETE FROM PM.P_INSC_XL WHERE INSC_ASET=ASSETOID;
COMMIT;
----------------------------------Update OID-------------------------------------
UPDATE PM.P_SEQ SET SEQ_NUMBER=TO_NUMBER(SEQ_NUMBER) + 1 WHERE SEQ_TEXT = '40';
COMMIT;
END IF;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_INSC_XL_JUNK_CLEAR  (Procedure) 
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_INSC_XL_JUNK_CLEAR
AS
BEGIN
----12-Dec-2019
DELETE FROM PM.P_INSC_XL X;
COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_LOV_PERMISSION  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_LOV_PERMISSION(
vLOV IN VARCHAR2,
vUSER IN VARCHAR2,
P_CURSOR IN OUT TYPE_CURSOR.cursorType)
IS

BEGIN
IF vLOV='MAIN_MENU'  THEN
    OPEN P_CURSOR FOR 
select sysdate from dual;
END IF;

EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
 NULL;
END; 
/

END IF;

EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
 NULL;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_SUM_FIVE  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_SUM_FIVE(USERID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
/*Staff Data Summery Generator -KSA*/

VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_T NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT COUNT(OWNR_ID) INTO VALUE_A FROM A_OWNR WHERE OWNR_TYPE='Staff' AND OWNR_POST='1' AND ACT='1';
SELECT COUNT(OWNR_ID) INTO VALUE_B FROM A_OWNR WHERE OWNR_TYPE='Staff' AND OWNR_POST='0' AND ACT='1';
SELECT COUNT(OWNR_ID) INTO VALUE_C FROM A_OWNR WHERE OWNR_TYPE='Staff'  AND ACT='0';
SELECT COUNT(OWNR_ID) INTO VALUE_T FROM A_OWNR WHERE OWNR_TYPE='Staff'  AND ACT='1';
UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C,DATA_J=VALUE_T,UDU=USERID,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE)
WHERE SV_TABLE='PRO_SUM_FIVE';
COMMIT;
END;

COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_USER_MENU  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_USER_MENU(
V_USER IN VARCHAR2,
P_CURSOR IN OUT TYPE_CURSOR.cursorType)
IS
BEGIN
OPEN P_CURSOR FOR 

SELECT P.PMNU_TEXT, P.PMNU_NAME, P.PMNU_ICON, C.CMNU_TEXT,
C.CMNU_NAME, C.CMNU_ICON, C.CMNU_NOTE,
CASE
WHEN U.ACT = '1'
THEN 'T'
WHEN U.ACT = '0'
THEN 'F'
ELSE 'N'
END ISOK
FROM A_MENUC C INNER JOIN A_MENUP P ON C.CMNU_PMNU = P.PMNU_TEXT
LEFT OUTER JOIN A_USERS_MENUC U
ON C.CMNU_TEXT = U.CMNU_TEXT AND U.USER_TEXT ='78278'
WHERE C.CMNU_NOTE = 'KSA' AND C.ACT = 1
ORDER BY ISOK DESC, P.PMNU_TEXT ASC, C.CMNU_TEXT ASC;

EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
 NULL;
END; 
/


--
-- P_ASET_ACTV  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   DBMS_OUTPUT (Synonym)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.P_ASET_ACTV (ASET_ID IN NUMBER,DAMAGED IN NUMBER)
AS

--Date: 04-Dec-2019 Name: Kayesh
   BEGIN
    IF DAMAGED=1
        THEN   
            UPDATE PM.P_ASSET A SET A.ASET_ACTV=1 WHERE A.ASET_OID=ASET_ID;
            COMMIT;
    ELSIF DAMAGED=0
        THEN
            UPDATE PM.P_ASSET A SET A.ASET_ACTV=0 WHERE A.ASET_OID=ASET_ID;
            COMMIT;
    ELSE
       DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Not Understood');
    END IF;
   END; 
/


--
-- P_ASET_CHANGE  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.P_ASET_CHANGE (ASET_ID IN NUMBER,DAMAGED IN VARCHAR2,OWNR VARCHAR2,RDAT Date, Notes VARCHAR2)
AS
--Date: 04-Dec-2019 Name: Kayesh
--ownership, lost, sales
   BEGIN
    UPDATE PM.P_ASSET SET ASET_ACTV='0', ASET_CLSE=DAMAGED WHERE ASET_OID=ASET_ID;
    COMMIT;
    UPDATE PM.P_OWNR SET OWNR_ACTV='0',OWNR_RELASE=RDAT,OWNR_NOTE=Notes WHERE OWNR_OID=OWNR;
    commit;
   END; 
/


--
-- P_ISBOOKED_BOOKING  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   DBMS_OUTPUT (Synonym)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.P_ISBOOKED_BOOKING (ASET_ID IN NUMBER,BOOKING IN NUMBER)
AS

--Date: 28-Nov-2019 Name: Kayesh
   BEGIN
    IF BOOKING=1
        THEN   
            UPDATE PM.P_ASSET A SET A.ISBOOKED=1 WHERE A.ASET_OID=ASET_ID;
            COMMIT;
    ELSIF BOOKING=0
        THEN
            UPDATE PM.P_ASSET A SET A.ISBOOKED=0 WHERE A.ASET_OID=ASET_ID;
            COMMIT;
    ELSE
       DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Not Understood');
    END IF;
   END; 
/


--
-- DUE_LIST  (View) 
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.DUE_LIST
(CASEDATE, PMNO, CASENO)
AS 
SELECT ALL PM.T_WORKORDERS.CASEDATE, 
PM.T_WORKORDERS.PMNO, PM.T_WORKORDERS.CASENO 
FROM PM.T_WORKORDERS;


--
-- PM_VCHL1  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.PM_VCHL1
(VHCL_ID)
AS 
SELECT PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID  FROM PM.PM_VHCL WHERE PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID NOT IN(SELECT NVL(PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID,'O') VHCL_ID  FROM PM.PM_OWNR) GROUP BY PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID UNION ALL SELECT PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID 
FROM PM.PM_OWNR WHERE OWNR_TDAT IS NOT NULL OR OWNR_RSGN='Y' GROUP BY PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID 
MINUS SELECT PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID  FROM PM.PM_OWNR WHERE OWNR_TDAT IS NULL GROUP BY PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID;


--
-- PM_V_DEED  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.PM_V_DEED
(DEPOT_NAME, CMP, VHCL_ID, VHCL_NAME, VHCL_PLRD, 
 OWNR_ID, OWNR_NAME, OWNR_ZONE)
AS 
SELECT 
    "PM_DEPOT"."DEPOT_NAME", "PM_DEPOT"."CMP", "PM_VHCL"."VHCL_ID", "PM_VHCL"."VHCL_NAME", "PM_VHCL"."VHCL_PLRD", "PM_OWNR"."OWNR_ID", "PM_OWNR"."OWNR_NAME", "PM_OWNR"."OWNR_ZONE" 
FROM 
    "PM"."PM_DEPOT" "PM_DEPOT", 
    "PM"."PM_VHCL" "PM_VHCL", 
    "PM"."PM_OWNR" "PM_OWNR" 
WHERE 
    "PM_DEPOT"."DEPOT_ID" = "PM_VHCL"."DEPOT_ID" AND 
    "PM_VHCL"."VHCL_ID" = "PM_OWNR"."VHCL_ID" AND 
    "PM_DEPOT"."CMP" = 'PRAN' AND 
    ("PM_DEPOT"."DEPOT_NAME" <> 'PRAN Sales-Mob Register' AND 
    "PM_DEPOT"."DEPOT_NAME" <> 'Refrigerator-Dairy') 
	 AND 
	 OWNR_DEDT IS NULL AND 
	 OWNR_TDAT IS NULL 
ORDER BY 
    "PM_DEPOT"."DEPOT_NAME" DESC;


--
-- SA  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.SA
(DEPOT_NAME, VHCL_ID, VHCL_NAME, OWNR_ID, OWNR_NAME, 
 OWNR_AREA, OWNR_ZONE, OWNR_RGON, VHCL_PLRD, CMP)
AS 
SELECT ALL PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME,
PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_AREA,
PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ZONE, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_RGON, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLRD,PM.PM_DEPOT.CMP
FROM PM.PM_OWNR, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_DEPOT
WHERE PM.PM_VHCL.TEXT='Sales'
 AND  ((PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID)
 AND (VHCL_CLSE='N')
 AND OWNR_TDAT IS NULL
 AND (PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID=PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID)
 AND (PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLRD is not null));


--
-- VW_ADT_CG_AUDITOR_RPT  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_REPORT (Table)
--   AUDIT_TASK (Table)
--   C_COMPANY_GROUP (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_ADT_CG_AUDITOR_RPT
(COMPANY_GROUP_ID, COMPANY_GROUP_NAME, CDU, TOTAL_NO_OF_REPORT, LAST_MONTH_TOTAL_REPORT, 
 THIS_MONTH_TOTAL_REPORT, TOTAL_WITH_REPORT, LAST_MONTH_WITH_REPORT, THIS_MONTH_WITH_REPORT, TOTAL_WITHOUT_REPORT, 
 LAST_MONTH_WITHOUT_REPORT, THIS_MONTH_WITHOUT_REPORT)
AS 
SELECT
    CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID ,
    CG.COMPANY_GROUP_NAME,
    R.CDU,
    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID IN (1,2,3,4) 
              THEN 1 
         END) AS TOTAL_NO_OF_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID IN (1,2,3,4)
                   AND R.CDT >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -1)
                   AND R.CDT < TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
              THEN 1 
         END) AS LAST_MONTH_TOTAL_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID IN (1,2,3,4)
                   AND R.CDT >= TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
                   AND R.CDT < ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), 1)
              THEN 1 
         END) AS THIS_MONTH_TOTAL_REPORT,
    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
              THEN 1 
         END) AS TOTAL_WITH_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.CDT >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -1)
                   AND R.CDT < TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
              THEN 1 
         END) AS LAST_MONTH_WITH_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.CDT >= TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
                   AND R.CDT < ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), 1)
              THEN 1 
         END) AS THIS_MONTH_WITH_REPORT,
    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
              THEN 1 
         END) AS TOTAL_WITHOUT_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.CDT >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -1)
                   AND R.CDT < TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
              THEN 1 
         END) AS LAST_MONTH_WITHOUT_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.CDT >= TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
                   AND R.CDT < ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), 1)
              THEN 1 
         END) AS THIS_MONTH_WITHOUT_REPORT


FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT 
    ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG 
    ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
WHERE R.ACT = 1  
 GROUP BY CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID, CG.COMPANY_GROUP_NAME, R.CDU
ORDER BY CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID;


--
-- VW_ADT_COM_GROUP_RPT  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_REPORT (Table)
--   AUDIT_TASK (Table)
--   C_COMPANY_GROUP (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_ADT_COM_GROUP_RPT
(COMPANY_GROUP_ID, COMPANY_GROUP_NAME, TOTAL_NO_OF_REPORT, LAST_MONTH_TOTAL_REPORT, THIS_MONTH_TOTAL_REPORT, 
 TOTAL_WITH_REPORT, LAST_MONTH_WITH_REPORT, THIS_MONTH_WITH_REPORT, TOTAL_WITHOUT_REPORT, LAST_MONTH_WITHOUT_REPORT, 
 THIS_MONTH_WITHOUT_REPORT)
AS 
SELECT
    CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID ,
    CG.COMPANY_GROUP_NAME ,
    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID IN (1,2,3,4) 
              THEN 1 
         END) AS TOTAL_NO_OF_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID IN (1,2,3,4)
                   AND R.CDT >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -1)
                   AND R.CDT < TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
              THEN 1 
         END) AS LAST_MONTH_TOTAL_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID IN (1,2,3,4)
                   AND R.CDT >= TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
                   AND R.CDT < ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), 1)
              THEN 1 
         END) AS THIS_MONTH_TOTAL_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
              THEN 1 
         END) AS TOTAL_WITH_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.CDT >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -1)
                   AND R.CDT < TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
              THEN 1 
         END) AS LAST_MONTH_WITH_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 1,3 )
                   AND R.CDT >= TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
                   AND R.CDT < ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), 1)
              THEN 1 
         END) AS THIS_MONTH_WITH_REPORT,
    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
              THEN 1 
         END) AS TOTAL_WITHOUT_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.CDT >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), -1)
                   AND R.CDT < TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
              THEN 1 
         END) AS LAST_MONTH_WITHOUT_REPORT,

    COUNT(CASE 
              WHEN AT.REPORT_CREATE_STATUS = 1 
                   AND AT.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.REPORT_STATUS_ID in ( 2,4 )
                   AND R.CDT >= TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM')
                   AND R.CDT < ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE, 'MM'), 1)
              THEN 1 
         END) AS THIS_MONTH_WITHOUT_REPORT


FROM AUDIT_REPORT R
LEFT JOIN AUDIT_TASK AT 
    ON R.TASK_ID = AT.TASK_ID
INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG 
    ON CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = AT.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
WHERE R.ACT = 1
GROUP BY CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID, CG.COMPANY_GROUP_NAME 
ORDER BY CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID;


--
-- VW_ADT_FST_FLW_UP_DAY_BEFORE  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_AUDITOR (Table)
--   AUDIT_REPORT (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_ADT_FST_FLW_UP_DAY_BEFORE
(ID, MAIL_TO, MAIL_CC, MAIL_BCC, MAIL_SUBJECT, 
 MAIL_BODY, ATTACHMENT_PATH, REFNO, TRN_DATE)
AS 
SELECT    
           AR.AUDIT_RPT_ID                                  AS ID,
           A.AUDITOR_EMAIL                                  AS MAIL_TO,
           ''                                               AS MAIL_CC,
           ''                                               AS MAIL_BCC,
           'Reminder: Audit First Follow-Up Required.'              AS MAIL_SUBJECT,
           
           '<p>Dear Sir,</p>
       This is a reminder that your Audit Report Number : <strong>'
           || AR.AUDIT_RPT_ID 
           || '</strong> requires a follow-up after <b style="color: red;">'
           || NVL ( TO_DATE (AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_DATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy') - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy'),0) 
           || '</b> day.<br>
       <p></p>
       <p>Please find the details below:</p>
       <table border="1" cellpadding="5" cellspacing="0">
            <tr>
                <th width="80">Report ID</th>
                <th width="120">Report Name</th>
                <th width="100">Task ID</th>
                <th width="100">Follow Up Date</th>
                <th width="180">Auditor Staff ID</th>
                <th width="100">Auditor Name</th>
                <th>Remaining (Days)</th>
            </tr>
            <tr>
                <td>'
           || AR.AUDIT_RPT_ID 
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || AR.AUDIT_RPT_NAME
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || AR.TASK_ID
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_DATE
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || AR.CDU  
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || A.AUDITOR_NAME
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           ||  NVL (
                 TO_DATE (AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_DATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
               - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy'),
               0)   
           || '</td>
            </tr>
        </table><br>
        <p></P>
        <p>Please take the necessary actions.<br></p><br>
        Thanks and Regards,<br><b>PRAN RFL Group</b><br>'    AS MAIL_BODY,
           
           
          ''                                               ATTACHMENT_PATH,
            AR.TASK_ID                                            AS REFNO,
           ''                                               TRN_DATE
      FROM AUDIT_REPORT  AR
           LEFT JOIN AUDIT_AUDITOR A ON AR.CDU = A.AUDITOR_STAFF_ID
     WHERE     AR.RPT_SENT_DATE IS NOT NULL
           AND AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_STATUS = 'N'
           AND AR.ACT = 1
           AND SYSDATE < AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_DATE
           AND AR.REPORT_STATUS_ID = 1
           AND AR.MANAGEMENT_ACTION_ID NOT IN (4)
           AND NVL (
                     TO_DATE (AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_DATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy'),
                   0) =
               1;


--
-- VW_ADT_FST_FLW_UP_EXPIRE_LIST  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_AUDITOR (Table)
--   AUDIT_REPORT (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_ADT_FST_FLW_UP_EXPIRE_LIST
(AUDIT_RPT_ID, AUDIT_RPT_NAME, TASK_ID, FIRST_FOLLOWUP_DATE, AUDITOR_STAFF_ID, 
 AUDITOR_NAME, DAYS_OVER)
AS 
SELECT 
    AR.AUDIT_RPT_ID,
     AR.AUDIT_RPT_NAME, 
    AR.TASK_ID, 
    AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_DATE,
    AR.CDU AS AUDITOR_STAFF_ID,
    A.AUDITOR_NAME,
    NVL (TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy') - TO_DATE (AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_DATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy') , 0)    AS DAYS_OVER
FROM 
    AUDIT_REPORT AR  
    LEFT JOIN AUDIT_AUDITOR A ON AR.CDU = A.AUDITOR_STAFF_ID
WHERE 
    AR.RPT_SENT_DATE IS NOT NULL 
    AND AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_STATUS = 'N' 
    AND AR.ACT = 1 
    AND SYSDATE  >  AR.FIRST_FOLLOWUP_DATE
    AND AR.REPORT_STATUS_ID = 1
    AND AR.MANAGEMENT_ACTION_ID NOT IN (4)
    Order By AUDITOR_NAME;


--
-- VW_INS_EXPIRE_DATE_COUNT  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   INS_INSURANCE_MST (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_INS_EXPIRE_DATE_COUNT
(INSURANCE_NUMBER, INSURANE_DATE, INSURANCE_EXPAIR_DATE, REMAINING)
AS 
select INS.INSURANCE_NUMBER,INS.INSURANE_DATE, INS.INSURANCE_EXPAIR_DATE, TO_DATE (INS.INSURANCE_EXPAIR_DATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
             - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy') 
                 AS Remaining  from INS_INSURANCE_MST INS WHERE INS.ACT=1;


--
-- VW_KSA_IQUMA_S1  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_KSA_IQUMA_S1
(OWNR_ID, OWNR_TEXT, OWNR_USER, OWNR_NAME, OWNR_NOTE, 
 AAJ_DATE, OWNR_NDATE, REMIND, DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, GROUP_NAME, 
 DAYS)
AS 
SELECT O.OWNR_ID,O.OWNR_TEXT,O.OWNR_USER,O.OWNR_NAME,O.OWNR_NOTE,TO_DATE(SYSDATE,'DD-MM-YYYY') AS AAJ_DATE,
O.OWNR_NDATE,O.REMIND,D.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS,
 TO_CHAR('GR0001') AS GROUP_NAME,
 TO_DATE (O.OWNR_NDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')- TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')+ 1 AS DAYS
FROM A_OWNR O
INNER JOIN A_DEPARTMENT D ON O.OWNR_DEPT=D.DEPARTMENT_ID
WHERE O.OWNR_TYPE='Staff'
AND O.OWNR_RESIGN='N'
AND O.ACT='1'
ORDER BY DAYS;


--
-- VW_KSA_IQUMA_S2  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   VW_KSA_IQUMA_S1 (View)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_KSA_IQUMA_S2
(OWNR_ID, OWNR_TEXT, OWNR_USER, OWNR_NAME, OWNR_NOTE, 
 AAJ_DATE, OWNR_NDATE, REMIND, DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, GROUP_NAME, 
 DAYS, MESSAGES, NEED_MAIL, MAIL_SUBJECT, MAIL_BODY, 
 UPDT)
AS 
SELECT u.ownr_id, u.ownr_text, u.ownr_user, u.ownr_name, u.ownr_note,
          u.aaj_date, u.ownr_ndate, u.remind, u.department_business,
          u.group_name, u.days,
          CASE
             WHEN u.days BETWEEN 31 AND 92
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 1
                          THEN 'This is First Mail'
                       ELSE 'First Reminder'
                    END
             WHEN u.days BETWEEN 1 AND 30
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 2
                          THEN 'This is Final Mail'
                       ELSE 'Final Reminder'
                    END
             WHEN u.days < 0
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 1
                          THEN 'This is First and Final Mail'
                       ELSE 'Expired'
                    END
             ELSE 'Unknown'
          END messages,
          CASE
             WHEN u.days BETWEEN 31 AND 92
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 1
                          THEN '1'
                       ELSE '0'
                    END
             WHEN u.days BETWEEN 1 AND 30
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 2
                          THEN '1'
                       ELSE '0'
                    END
             WHEN u.days < 0
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 1
                          THEN '1'
                       ELSE '0'
                    END
             ELSE '0'
          END need_mail,
          'Iqama' || '-' || u.ownr_name || '-' || u.ownr_user AS mail_subject,
          '<p>Dear Concern,</p><p>Your document will expire soon, Please take necessary action-</p><p>Staff Id: <strong>{0}</strong></p><p>Staff Name: <strong>{1}</strong> </p><p>Note: <strong>{2}</strong> </p><p>Iqama No: <strong>{3}</strong> </p><p>Notification Messages: <strong>{4}</strong> </p><p>Mail Date: <strong>{5}</strong></p><p>Expire Date: <strong>{6}</strong></p><p>Remining Day: <strong>{7}</strong> Days</p><p>Thank you</p><hr/><p>This is a system generated email, do not reply to this email id.</p>'
                                                                 AS mail_body,
          'update a_ownr x set x.REMIND=x.REMIND+1 where x.OWNR_ID' AS updt
     FROM vw_ksa_iquma_s1 u
    WHERE u.days < 92;


--
-- VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATA_PRAN  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ITEM_MASTER (Table)
--   C_COMPANY_GROUP (Table)
--   LIC_COMPANY (Table)
--   LIC_ISSUING_AUTHORITY (Table)
--   LIC_LICENSE (Table)
--   LIC_LICENSE_NAME (Table)
--   LIC_LOCATION (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATA_PRAN
("LOCATION NAME", "COMPANY NAME", "LICENSE NAME", "AUTHORITY NAME", "LICENSE NUMBER", 
 "ISSUE DATE", "EXPIRE DATE", REMARK, "LICENSE HOLDER EMAIL", "LICENSE HOLDER MOBILE", 
 "DAYS OVER")
AS 
SELECT 
    LO.LOCATION_NAME AS "LOCATION NAME",
    C.COMPANY_NAME AS "COMPANY NAME",
    LN.LICENSE_NAME AS "LICENSE NAME",
    LIA.AUTHORITY_NAME AS "AUTHORITY NAME",
    L.LICENSE_NUMBER AS "LICENSE NUMBER",
    TO_CHAR(L.ISSUE_DATE, 'DD-MM-YYYY') AS "ISSUE DATE",
    TO_CHAR(L.EXPIRE_DATE, 'DD-MM-YYYY') AS "EXPIRE DATE",
    L.REMARK,
    L.LICENSE_HOLDER_EMAIL AS "LICENSE HOLDER EMAIL",
    L.LICENSE_HOLDER_MOBILE AS "LICENSE HOLDER MOBILE",
     NVL(TRUNC(SYSDATE - L.EXPIRE_DATE), 0) 
    AS "DAYS OVER"
FROM LIC_LICENSE L
LEFT JOIN LIC_LOCATION LO 
    ON L.LIC_LOCATION_ID = LO.LIC_LOCATION_ID
LEFT JOIN LIC_LICENSE_NAME LN 
    ON L.LICENSE_NAME_ID = LN.LICENSE_NAME_ID
LEFT JOIN LIC_ISSUING_AUTHORITY LIA 
    ON L.ISSUING_AUTHORITY_ID = LIA.ISSUING_AUTHORITY_ID
LEFT JOIN A_ITEM_MASTER I 
    ON L.ITEM_ID = I.ITEM_ID
LEFT JOIN LIC_COMPANY C 
    ON L.COMPANY_ID = C.COMPANY_ID
INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG 
    ON C.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
WHERE 
    L.LICENSE_STATUS_ID = 1
    AND C.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 1
    AND L.ACT = 1
    AND NVL(TRUNC(SYSDATE - L.EXPIRE_DATE), 0) >= 0
    AND LN.LICENSE_TYPE_ID = 2
ORDER BY 
    LO.LOCATION_NAME ASC, 
    C.COMPANY_NAME ASC;


--
-- VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATA_RFL  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ITEM_MASTER (Table)
--   C_COMPANY_GROUP (Table)
--   LIC_COMPANY (Table)
--   LIC_ISSUING_AUTHORITY (Table)
--   LIC_LICENSE (Table)
--   LIC_LICENSE_NAME (Table)
--   LIC_LOCATION (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATA_RFL
("LOCATION NAME", "COMPANY NAME", "LICENSE NAME", "AUTHORITY NAME", "LICENSE NUMBER", 
 "ISSUE DATE", "EXPIRE DATE", REMARK, "LICENSE HOLDER EMAIL", "LICENSE HOLDER MOBILE", 
 "DAYS OVER")
AS 
SELECT LO.LOCATION_NAME
                 AS "LOCATION NAME",
             C.COMPANY_NAME
                 AS "COMPANY NAME",
             LN.LICENSE_NAME
                 AS "LICENSE NAME",
             LIA.AUTHORITY_NAME
                 AS "AUTHORITY NAME",
             L.LICENSE_NUMBER
                 AS "LICENSE NUMBER",
             TO_CHAR (L.ISSUE_DATE, 'DD-MM-YYYY')
                 AS "ISSUE DATE",
             TO_CHAR (L.EXPIRE_DATE, 'DD-MM-YYYY')
                 AS "EXPIRE DATE",
             L.REMARK,
             L.LICENSE_HOLDER_EMAIL
                 AS "LICENSE HOLDER EMAIL",
             L.LICENSE_HOLDER_MOBILE
                 AS "LICENSE HOLDER MOBILE",
             NVL (TRUNC (SYSDATE - L.EXPIRE_DATE), 0)
                 AS "DAYS OVER"
        FROM LIC_LICENSE L
             LEFT JOIN LIC_LOCATION LO
                 ON L.LIC_LOCATION_ID = LO.LIC_LOCATION_ID
             LEFT JOIN LIC_LICENSE_NAME LN
                 ON L.LICENSE_NAME_ID = LN.LICENSE_NAME_ID
             LEFT JOIN LIC_ISSUING_AUTHORITY LIA
                 ON L.ISSUING_AUTHORITY_ID = LIA.ISSUING_AUTHORITY_ID
             LEFT JOIN A_ITEM_MASTER I ON L.ITEM_ID = I.ITEM_ID
             LEFT JOIN LIC_COMPANY C ON L.COMPANY_ID = C.COMPANY_ID
             INNER JOIN C_COMPANY_GROUP CG
                 ON C.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
       WHERE     L.LICENSE_STATUS_ID = 1
             AND C.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 2
             AND L.ACT = 1
             AND NVL (TRUNC (SYSDATE - L.EXPIRE_DATE), 0) >= 0
             AND LN.LICENSE_TYPE_ID = 2
    ORDER BY LO.LOCATION_NAME ASC, C.COMPANY_NAME ASC;


--
-- VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATE_COUNT  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ITEM_MASTER (Table)
--   LIC_ISSUING_AUTHORITY (Table)
--   LIC_LICENSE (Table)
--   LIC_LICENSE_NAME (Table)
--   LIC_LOCATION (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATE_COUNT
(LICENSE_ID, LICENSE_NUMBER, ISSUE_DATE, EXPIRE_DATE, REMAINING, 
 LICENSE_HOLDER_EMAIL, LOCATION_NAME, LICENSE_NAME, AUTHORITY_NAME, ITEM_NAME)
AS 
SELECT       L.LICENSE_ID,
             L.LICENSE_NUMBER,
             L.ISSUE_DATE,
             L.EXPIRE_DATE,
             NVL (
                   TO_DATE (EXPIRE_DATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                 - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy'),
                 0)    AS REMAINING,
             L.LICENSE_HOLDER_EMAIL,
             LO.LOCATION_NAME,
             LN.LICENSE_NAME,
             LIA.AUTHORITY_NAME,
             I.ITEM_NAME
        FROM LIC_LICENSE L
        LEFT JOIN LIC_LOCATION LO ON L.LIC_LOCATION_ID = LO.LIC_LOCATION_ID 
        LEFT JOIN LIC_LICENSE_NAME LN ON  L.LICENSE_NAME_ID = LN.LICENSE_NAME_ID
        LEFT JOIN LIC_ISSUING_AUTHORITY LIA ON  L.ISSUING_AUTHORITY_ID = LIA.ISSUING_AUTHORITY_ID
        LEFT JOIN A_ITEM_MASTER I ON  L.ITEM_ID = I.ITEM_ID
        LEFT JOIN LIC_LICENSE_NAME LN ON L.LICENSE_NAME_ID = LN.LICENSE_NAME_ID
        WHERE L.ACT=1 
        AND L.LICENSE_STATUS_ID =1 AND LN.LICENSE_TYPE_ID = 2
    ORDER BY REMAINING;


--
-- VW_LIC_EXPIRE_NOTIFY_MAIL  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATE_COUNT (View)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_LIC_EXPIRE_NOTIFY_MAIL
(ID, MAIL_TO, MAIL_CC, MAIL_BCC, MAIL_SUBJECT, 
 MAIL_BODY, ATTACHMENT_PATH, REFNO, TRN_DATE)
AS 
SELECT LIC.LICENSE_ID                                   AS ID,
           LIC.LICENSE_HOLDER_EMAIL                         AS MAIL_TO,
           ''                                               AS MAIL_CC,
           ''                                               AS MAIL_BCC,
           'License Expiry Notification'                    AS MAIL_SUBJECT,
           
           
              '<p>Dear Sir,</p>
       This is a reminder that your license number : <strong>'
           || LIC.LICENSE_NUMBER
           || '</strong> will expire after <b style="color: red;">'
           || LIC.REMAINING
           || '</b> days.<br>
       <p></p>
       <p>Please find the details below:</p>
       <table border="1" cellpadding="5" cellspacing="0">
            <tr>
                <th width="80">License ID</th>
                <th width="100">Location </th>
                <th width="120">License Name</th>
                <th width="100">Item Name</th>
                <th width="100">Issuing Authority</th>
                <th width="180">License Number</th>
                <th width="100">Issue Date</th>
                <th width="100">Expire Date</th>
                <th>Remaining (Days)</th>
            </tr>
            <tr>
                <td>'
           || LIC.LICENSE_ID
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || LIC.LOCATION_NAME
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || LIC.LICENSE_NAME
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || LIC.ITEM_NAME
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || LIC.AUTHORITY_NAME
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || LIC.LICENSE_NUMBER
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || TO_CHAR (LIC.ISSUE_DATE, 'DD-MM-YYYY')
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || TO_CHAR (LIC.EXPIRE_DATE, 'DD-MM-YYYY')
           || '</td>
                <td>'
           || LIC.REMAINING
           || '</td>
            </tr>
        </table><br>
        <p></P>
        <p>Please take the necessary actions.<br></p><br>
        Thanks and Regards,<br><b>PRAN RFL Group</b><br>'    AS MAIL_BODY,
        
           ''                                               ATTACHMENT_PATH,
           LIC.LICENSE_NUMBER                               AS REFNO,
           ''                                               TRN_DATE
      FROM VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATE_COUNT LIC
     WHERE LIC.REMAINING IN (45, 30, 7);


--
-- VW_RPT_JOBD_S1  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ITEM_LIST (Table)
--   A_JOBD (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_RPT_JOBD_S1
(JOBH_ID, JOBD_LINE, JOBD_ITEM, ITEM_NAME, JOBD_IQTY, 
 JOBD_IRATE, JOBD_IAMNT, JOBD_JQTY, JOBD_JRATE, JOBD_JAMNT, 
 JOBD_NOTE)
AS 
SELECT   JD.JOBH_ID, JD.JOBD_LINE, JD.JOBD_ITEM, I.ITEM_NAME, JD.JOBD_IQTY,
         JD.JOBD_IRATE, SUM (JD.JOBD_IQTY * JD.JOBD_IRATE) AS JOBD_IAMNT,
         JD.JOBD_JQTY, JD.JOBD_JRATE,
         SUM (JD.JOBD_JQTY * JD.JOBD_JRATE) AS JOBD_JAMNT, JD.JOBD_NOTE
    FROM A_JOBD JD INNER JOIN A_ITEM_LIST I ON JD.JOBD_ITEM = I.ITEM_ID
GROUP BY JD.JOBH_ID,
         JD.JOBD_LINE,
         JD.JOBD_ITEM,
         I.ITEM_NAME,
         JD.JOBD_IQTY,
         JD.JOBD_IRATE,
         JD.JOBD_JQTY,
         JD.JOBD_JRATE,
         JD.JOBD_NOTE
ORDER BY JD.JOBH_ID;


--
-- VW_RPT_JOB_SUM  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_JOBD (Table)
--   A_JOBH (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_RPT_JOB_SUM
(JOBH_ID, INSPH_ID, JOBH_ASET, JOBH_DATE, JOBH_AMT)
AS 
select JH.jobh_id,jh.insph_id,jh.jobh_aset,jh.jobh_date,sum(jd.jobd_jqty*jd.jobd_jrate) as JOBH_AMT
from a_jobh jh
inner join a_jobd jd on jh.jobh_id=jd.jobh_id
group by jh.jobh_id,jh.INSPH_ID,jh.JOBH_ASET,jh.jobh_date
order by jh.jobh_aset;


--
-- V_AUDIT_REPORT_FOLLOWUP  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   AUDIT_FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT (Table)
--   AUDIT_REPORT_FOLLOWUP (Table)
--   AUDIT_RISK_MITIGATION (Table)
--   AUDIT_RISK_TYPE (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_AUDIT_REPORT_FOLLOWUP
(AUDIT_RPT_ID, FU1ST_FOLLOWUP_NUMBER, FU1ST_AUDIT_RPT_FOLLOWUP_ID, FU1ST_FOLLOWUP_DATE, FU1ST_TOTAL_FINDINGS, 
 FU1ST_ACTION_FINDINGS, FU1ST_NO_ACTION_FINDINGS, FU1ST_ACTION_PERCENTAGE, FU1ST_RISK_TYPE_ID, FU1ST_RISK_TYPE_NAME, 
 FU1ST_RISK_MITIGATION_ID, FU1ST_RISK_MITIGATION_NAME, FU1ST_RISK_MITIGATION_MARKS, FU1ST_REPLY_PERSON, FU1ST_RESPONSIBLE_DEPT_HEAD, 
 FU1ST_REMARKS, FU1ST_SIGNIFICANT_GAP, FU1ST_FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID, FU1ST_FU_REQUIREMENT_NAME, FU1ST_CDT, 
 FU2ND_FOLLOWUP_NUMBER, FU2ND_AUDIT_RPT_FOLLOWUP_ID, FU2ND_FOLLOWUP_DATE, FU2ND_TOTAL_FINDINGS, FU2ND_ACTION_FINDINGS, 
 FU2ND_NO_ACTION_FINDINGS, FU2ND_ACTION_PERCENTAGE, FU2ND_RISK_TYPE_ID, FU2ND_RISK_TYPE_NAME, FU2ND_RISK_MITIGATION_ID, 
 FU2ND_RISK_MITIGATION_NAME, FU2ND_RISK_MITIGATION_MARKS, FU2ND_REPLY_PERSON, FU2ND_RESPONSIBLE_DEPT_HEAD, FU2ND_REMARKS, 
 FU2ND_SIGNIFICANT_GAP, FU2ND_FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID, FU2ND_FU_REQUIREMENT_NAME, FU2ND_CDT, FU3RD_FOLLOWUP_NUMBER, 
 FU3RD_AUDIT_RPT_FOLLOWUP_ID, FU3RD_FOLLOWUP_DATE, FU3RD_TOTAL_FINDINGS, FU3RD_ACTION_FINDINGS, FU3RD_NO_ACTION_FINDINGS, 
 FU3RD_ACTION_PERCENTAGE, FU3RD_RISK_TYPE_ID, FU3RD_RISK_TYPE_NAME, FU3RD_RISK_MITIGATION_ID, FU3RD_RISK_MITIGATION_NAME, 
 FU3RD_RISK_MITIGATION_MARKS, FU3RD_REPLY_PERSON, FU3RD_RESPONSIBLE_DEPT_HEAD, FU3RD_REMARKS, FU3RD_SIGNIFICANT_GAP, 
 FU3RD_FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID, FU3RD_FU_REQUIREMENT_NAME, FU3RD_CDT)
AS 
SELECT 
    RF.AUDIT_RPT_ID,
     
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER END) AS FU1ST_FOLLOWUP_NUMBER, 
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.AUDIT_RPT_FOLLOWUP_ID END) AS FU1ST_AUDIT_RPT_FOLLOWUP_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.FOLLOWUP_DATE END) AS FU1ST_FOLLOWUP_DATE,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.TOTAL_FINDINGS END) AS FU1ST_TOTAL_FINDINGS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.ACTION_FINDINGS END) AS FU1ST_ACTION_FINDINGS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.NO_ACTION_FINDINGS END) AS FU1ST_NO_ACTION_FINDINGS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.ACTION_PERCENTAGE END) AS FU1ST_ACTION_PERCENTAGE,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.RISK_TYPE_ID END) AS FU1ST_RISK_TYPE_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RT.RISK_TYPE_NAME END) AS FU1ST_RISK_TYPE_NAME,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.RISK_MITIGATION_ID END) AS FU1ST_RISK_MITIGATION_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RM.RISK_MITIGATION_NAME END) AS FU1ST_RISK_MITIGATION_NAME,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.RISK_MITIGATION_MARKS END) AS FU1ST_RISK_MITIGATION_MARKS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.REPLY_PERSON END) AS FU1ST_REPLY_PERSON,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.RESPONSIBLE_DEPT_HEAD END) AS FU1ST_RESPONSIBLE_DEPT_HEAD,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.REMARKS END) AS FU1ST_REMARKS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.SIGNIFICANT_GAP END) AS FU1ST_SIGNIFICANT_GAP,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID END) AS FU1ST_FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN FUR.FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_NAME END) AS FU1ST_FU_REQUIREMENT_NAME,
     
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 1 THEN RF.CDT END) AS FU1ST_CDT,
    

    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER END) AS FU2ND_FOLLOWUP_NUMBER,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.AUDIT_RPT_FOLLOWUP_ID END) AS FU2ND_AUDIT_RPT_FOLLOWUP_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.FOLLOWUP_DATE END) AS FU2ND_FOLLOWUP_DATE,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.TOTAL_FINDINGS END) AS FU2ND_TOTAL_FINDINGS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.ACTION_FINDINGS END) AS FU2ND_ACTION_FINDINGS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.NO_ACTION_FINDINGS END) AS FU2ND_NO_ACTION_FINDINGS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.ACTION_PERCENTAGE END) AS FU2ND_ACTION_PERCENTAGE,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.RISK_TYPE_ID END) AS FU2ND_RISK_TYPE_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RT.RISK_TYPE_NAME END) AS FU2ND_RISK_TYPE_NAME,
      
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.RISK_MITIGATION_ID END) AS FU2ND_RISK_MITIGATION_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RM.RISK_MITIGATION_NAME END) AS FU2ND_RISK_MITIGATION_NAME,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.RISK_MITIGATION_MARKS END) AS FU2ND_RISK_MITIGATION_MARKS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.REPLY_PERSON END) AS FU2ND_REPLY_PERSON,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.RESPONSIBLE_DEPT_HEAD END) AS FU2ND_RESPONSIBLE_DEPT_HEAD,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.REMARKS END) AS FU2ND_REMARKS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.SIGNIFICANT_GAP END) AS FU2ND_SIGNIFICANT_GAP,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID END) AS FU2ND_FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN FUR.FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_NAME END) AS FU2ND_FU_REQUIREMENT_NAME,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 2 THEN RF.CDT END) AS FU2ND_CDT,

    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER END) AS FU3RD_FOLLOWUP_NUMBER,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.AUDIT_RPT_FOLLOWUP_ID END) AS FU3RD_AUDIT_RPT_FOLLOWUP_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.FOLLOWUP_DATE END) AS FU3RD_FOLLOWUP_DATE,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.TOTAL_FINDINGS END) AS FU3RD_TOTAL_FINDINGS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.ACTION_FINDINGS END) AS FU3RD_ACTION_FINDINGS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.NO_ACTION_FINDINGS END) AS FU3RD_NO_ACTION_FINDINGS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.ACTION_PERCENTAGE END) AS FU3RD_ACTION_PERCENTAGE,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.RISK_TYPE_ID END) AS FU3RD_RISK_TYPE_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RT.RISK_TYPE_NAME END) AS FU3RD_RISK_TYPE_NAME,
      
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.RISK_MITIGATION_ID END) AS FU3RD_RISK_MITIGATION_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RM.RISK_MITIGATION_NAME END) AS FU3RD_RISK_MITIGATION_NAME,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.RISK_MITIGATION_MARKS END) AS FU3RD_RISK_MITIGATION_MARKS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.REPLY_PERSON END) AS FU3RD_REPLY_PERSON,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.RESPONSIBLE_DEPT_HEAD END) AS FU3RD_RESPONSIBLE_DEPT_HEAD,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.REMARKS END) AS FU3RD_REMARKS,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.SIGNIFICANT_GAP END) AS FU3RD_SIGNIFICANT_GAP,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID END) AS FU3RD_FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID,
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN FUR.FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_NAME END) AS FU3RD_FU_REQUIREMENT_NAME,
    
    MAX(CASE WHEN RF.FOLLOWUP_NUMBER = 3 THEN RF.CDT END) AS FU3RD_CDT

FROM 
    AUDIT_REPORT_FOLLOWUP RF
    LEFT JOIN AUDIT_RISK_TYPE RT ON RF.RISK_TYPE_ID = RT.RISK_TYPE_ID
    LEFT JOIN AUDIT_RISK_MITIGATION RM ON RF.RISK_MITIGATION_ID = RM.RISK_MITIGATION_ID
    LEFT JOIN AUDIT_FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT FUR ON RF.FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID = FUR.FOLLOWUP_REQUIREMENT_ID 
WHERE 
    RF.ACT = 1
GROUP BY 
    RF.AUDIT_RPT_ID;


--
-- V_DOC_SHDL  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   DOC_SHDL (Table)
--   PM_CTFT (Table)
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_DOC_SHDL
(DEPOT_ID, DEPOT_NAME, VHCL_NAME, OWNR_NAME, CTFT_NAME, 
 OID, DOC_ID, DOC_PSTN, DOC_AMNT, DOC_YRLY, 
 DOC_YY, DOC_DUDT, DOC_DONE, DOC_CMDT, DOC_FMNT, 
 DOC_EXTR, DOC_PO, DDIFF, TEXT)
AS 
SELECT ALL  PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME,PM.PM_CTFT.CTFT_NAME, 
PM.DOC_SHDL.OID, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_ID, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_PSTN, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_AMNT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_YRLY, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_YY, 
PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DUDT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DONE,  PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_CMDT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_FMNT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_EXTR, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_PO, (TO_DATE(PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_CMDT,'DD-MM-YYYY') - TO_DATE(PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DUDT,'DD-MM-YYYY')) DDIFF, PM.DOC_SHDL.TEXT 
FROM PM.PM_DEPOT, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_OWNR,PM.PM_CTFT, PM.DOC_SHDL 
WHERE PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID= PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID 
AND PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID=PM.DOC_SHDL.VHCL_ID 
AND PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.OWNR_ID 
AND PM.PM_CTFT.OID_CTFT=PM.DOC_SHDL.OID_CTFT 
AND PM.DOC_SHDL.TEXT = 'Transport' 
GROUP BY PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME,PM.PM_CTFT.CTFT_NAME, 
PM.DOC_SHDL.OID, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_ID, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_PSTN, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_AMNT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_YRLY, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_YY, 
PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DUDT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DONE,  PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_CMDT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_FMNT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_EXTR, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_PO, PM.DOC_SHDL.TEXT;


--
-- V_DWSD  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   T_AREA (Table)
--   T_ICRG (Table)
--   T_LZON (Table)
--   T_MNTH (Table)
--   T_PRJT (Table)
--   T_SHDL (Table)
--   T_STAT (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_DWSD
(PRJT_NAME, PRJT_NUML, AREA_NAME, STAT_NAME, SHDL_DATE, 
 SHDL_ACDT, ICRG_NAME, ICRG_CONT, PRJT_CONP, PRJT_CONN, 
 AREA_LZON, SHDL_MNTH, LZON_NAME, MNTH_NAME)
AS 
SELECT T_PRJT.PRJT_NAME, T_PRJT.PRJT_NUML, T_AREA.AREA_NAME, T_STAT.STAT_NAME, T_SHDL.SHDL_DATE,T_SHDL.SHDL_ACDT, T_ICRG.ICRG_NAME,
T_ICRG.ICRG_CONT, T_PRJT.PRJT_CONP, T_PRJT.PRJT_CONN, T_AREA.AREA_LZON, T_SHDL.SHDL_MNTH, T_LZON.LZON_NAME, T_MNTH.MNTH_NAME
FROM PM.T_PRJT, PM.T_AREA, PM.T_STAT, PM.T_SHDL, PM.T_ICRG, PM.T_MNTH, T_LZON
WHERE T_PRJT.PRJT_AREA=T_AREA.OID
AND T_PRJT.PRJT_STAT=T_STAT.OID
AND T_ICRG.ICRG_LZON = T_AREA.AREA_LZON
AND T_SHDL.SHDL_PRJT=T_PRJT.OID
AND T_AREA.AREA_LZON=T_LZON.OID
AND T_SHDL.SHDL_MNTH=T_MNTH.OID;


--
-- V_E_DEP  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_E_DEP
(VHCL_ID, VHCL_PMNT, VHCL_RGDT, EMON, BALANCE, 
 DEPR, DEP_MONTH, DEPOT_ID)
AS 
SELECT vhcl_id, vhcl_pmnt, vhcl_rgdt, emon,
          DECODE (bl / ABS (bl), -1, 1, bl) balance,
          DECODE (bl / ABS (bl), -1, (vhcl_pmnt - 1), dep) depr,DEP_MONTH,DEPOT_ID
     FROM (SELECT vhcl_id, vhcl_pmnt, vhcl_rgdt,
                  ROUND ((SYSDATE - vhcl_rgdt) / 30) emon,
                  NVL (ROUND (  (NVL (vhcl_pmnt, 0) / DECODE(DEPOT_ID,'49055',24,'48503',18,'48502',36,48))
                              * (ROUND ((SYSDATE - vhcl_rgdt) / 30))
                             ),
                       0
                      ) dep,
                  DECODE (NVL (  vhcl_pmnt
                               - (ROUND (  (NVL (vhcl_pmnt, 0) / DECODE(DEPOT_ID,'49055',24,'48503',18,'48502',36,48))
                                         * (ROUND ((SYSDATE - vhcl_rgdt) / 30)
                                           )
                                        )
                                 ),
                               0
                              ),
                          0, 1,
                          NVL (  vhcl_pmnt
                               - (ROUND (  (NVL (vhcl_pmnt, 0) / DECODE(DEPOT_ID,'49055',24,'48503',18,'48502',36,48))
                                         * (ROUND ((SYSDATE - vhcl_rgdt) / 30)
                                           )
                                        )
                                 ),
                               0
                              )
                         ) bl,
                         DECODE(DEPOT_ID,'49055',24,'48503',18,'48502',36,48) DEP_MONTH, DEPOT_ID
             FROM pm.pm_vhcl
            WHERE
--DEPOT_ID = '48510' AND
--VHCL_PMNT >= 0
--AND
                  vhcl_clse = 'N' AND vhcl_rgdt IS NOT NULL
--and VHCL_PMNT-(ROUND((NVL(VHCL_PMNT,0)/48)*(ROUND((SYSDATE-VHCL_RGDT)/30))))=0
-- ORDER BY VHCL_RGDT
          );


--
-- V_PLDT_CHK  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_PLDT_CHK
(DEPOT_NAME, VHCL_ID, VHCL_NAME, OWNR_ID, OWNR_NAME, 
 OWNR_AREA, OWNR_ZONE, OWNR_RGON, VHCL_PLRD, CMP)
AS 
SELECT ALL PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME,
PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_AREA,
PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ZONE, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_RGON, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLRD,PM.PM_DEPOT.CMP
FROM PM.PM_OWNR, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_DEPOT
WHERE PM.PM_VHCL.TEXT='Sales'
 AND  ((PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID)
 AND (VHCL_CLSE='N')
 AND OWNR_TDAT IS NULL
 AND (PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID=PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID)
 AND (PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLRD is not null));


--
-- V_PLDT_CHK_OLD  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_PLDT_CHK_OLD
(DEPOT_NAME, VHCL_ID, VHCL_NAME, OWNR_ID, OWNR_NAME, 
 OWNR_AREA, OWNR_ZONE, OWNR_RGON, VHCL_PLRD)
AS 
SELECT ALL PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, 
PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_AREA, 
PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ZONE, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_RGON, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLRD 
FROM PM.PM_OWNR, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_DEPOT 
WHERE PM.PM_VHCL.TEXT='Sales' 
 AND  ((PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID) 
 AND (VHCL_CLSE='N') 
 AND OWNR_TDAT IS NULL 
 AND (PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID=PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID) 
 AND (PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLRD is not null));


--
-- V_PRJT  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   T_AREA (Table)
--   T_LZON (Table)
--   T_PRJT (Table)
--   T_STAT (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_PRJT
(PRJT_NAME, AREA_NAME, STAT_NAME, PRJT_ADDS, AREA_LZON, 
 LZON_NAME)
AS 
SELECT T_PRJT.PRJT_NAME, T_AREA.AREA_NAME, T_STAT.STAT_NAME, T_PRJT.PRJT_ADDS, T_AREA.AREA_LZON, T_LZON.LZON_NAME
FROM PM.T_PRJT, PM.T_AREA, PM.T_STAT, PM.T_LZON
WHERE T_PRJT.PRJT_AREA=T_AREA.OID
AND T_PRJT.PRJT_STAT=T_STAT.OID
AND T_AREA.AREA_LZON=T_LZON.OID
ORDER BY T_LZON.LZON_NAME asc, T_AREA.AREA_NAME asc, T_PRJT.PRJT_NAME asc;


--
-- V_PWSD  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   T_PRJT (Table)
--   T_SHDL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_PWSD
(PRJT_NAME, PRJT_OID, SHDL_DATE, SHDL_ACDT)
AS 
SELECT T_PRJT.PRJT_NAME, T_PRJT.OID, T_SHDL.SHDL_DATE,T_SHDL.SHDL_ACDT
FROM PM.T_PRJT, PM.T_SHDL
WHERE T_SHDL.SHDL_PRJT=T_PRJT.OID;


--
-- V_SCHEDULE  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   T_AREA (Table)
--   T_ICRG (Table)
--   T_LZON (Table)
--   T_MNTH (Table)
--   T_PRJT (Table)
--   T_SHDL (Table)
--   T_STAT (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_SCHEDULE
(PRJT_NAME, PRJT_NUML, AREA_NAME, STAT_NAME, SHDL_DATE, 
 SHDL_ACDT, ICRG_NAME, ICRG_CONT, PRJT_CONP, PRJT_CONN, 
 AREA_LZON, SHDL_MNTH, LZON_NAME, MNTH_NAME)
AS 
SELECT T_PRJT.PRJT_NAME, T_PRJT.PRJT_NUML, T_AREA.AREA_NAME, T_STAT.STAT_NAME, T_SHDL.SHDL_DATE,T_SHDL.SHDL_ACDT, T_ICRG.ICRG_NAME,
T_ICRG.ICRG_CONT, T_PRJT.PRJT_CONP, T_PRJT.PRJT_CONN, T_AREA.AREA_LZON, T_SHDL.SHDL_MNTH, T_LZON.LZON_NAME, T_MNTH.MNTH_NAME
FROM PM.T_PRJT, PM.T_AREA, PM.T_STAT, PM.T_SHDL, PM.T_ICRG, PM.T_MNTH, T_LZON
WHERE T_PRJT.PRJT_AREA=T_AREA.OID
AND T_PRJT.PRJT_STAT=T_STAT.OID
AND T_ICRG.ICRG_LZON = T_AREA.AREA_LZON
AND T_SHDL.SHDL_PRJT=T_PRJT.OID
AND T_AREA.AREA_LZON=T_LZON.OID
AND T_SHDL.SHDL_MNTH=T_MNTH.OID
ORDER BY T_SHDL.SHDL_DATE DESC;


--
-- V_S_DOC_SHDL  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   DOC_SHDL (Table)
--   PM_CTFT (Table)
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_S_DOC_SHDL
(DEPOT_ID, DEPOT_NAME, VHCL_NAME, OWNR_NAME, CTFT_NAME, 
 OID, DOC_ID, DOC_PSTN, DOC_AMNT, DOC_YRLY, 
 DOC_YY, DOC_DUDT, DOC_DONE, DOC_CMDT, DOC_FMNT, 
 DOC_EXTR, DOC_PO, DDIFF, TEXT)
AS 
SELECT ALL  PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME,PM.PM_CTFT.CTFT_NAME, 
PM.DOC_SHDL.OID, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_ID, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_PSTN, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_AMNT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_YRLY, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_YY, 
PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DUDT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DONE,  PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_CMDT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_FMNT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_EXTR, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_PO, (TO_DATE(PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_CMDT,'DD-MM-YYYY') - TO_DATE(PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DUDT,'DD-MM-YYYY')) DDIFF, PM.DOC_SHDL.TEXT 
FROM PM.PM_DEPOT, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_OWNR,PM.PM_CTFT, PM.DOC_SHDL 
WHERE PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID= PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID 
AND PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID=PM.DOC_SHDL.VHCL_ID 
AND PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID 
AND PM.PM_CTFT.OID_CTFT=PM.DOC_SHDL.OID_CTFT 
AND PM.DOC_SHDL.TEXT = 'Sales' 
GROUP BY PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME,PM.PM_CTFT.CTFT_NAME, 
PM.DOC_SHDL.OID, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_ID, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_PSTN, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_AMNT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_YRLY, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_YY, 
PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DUDT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_DONE,  PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_CMDT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_FMNT, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_EXTR, PM.DOC_SHDL.DOC_PO, PM.DOC_SHDL.TEXT;


--
-- V_S_VHCL_INFO  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_S_VHCL_INFO
(DEPOT_ID, DEPOT_NAME, VHCL_ID, VHCL_NAME, OWNR_ID, 
 OWNR_NAME, VHCL_MODL, VHCL_PMNT, VHCL_RGDT, VHCL_TYPE, 
 VHCL_ENGN, VHCL_PLNO, VHCL_NOTE, VHCL_CDTE, VHCL_CLSE)
AS 
SELECT ALL  PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_MODL, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PMNT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_RGDT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_TYPE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ENGN, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLNO, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NOTE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_CDTE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_CLSE
FROM PM.PM_DEPOT, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_OWNR
WHERE (PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID= PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID) 
AND (PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.OWNR_ID) 
AND (PM.PM_VHCL.TEXT='Sales') 
AND (PM.PM_DEPOT.TEXT='Sales')
AND (PM.PM_OWNR.TEXT='Sales')
GROUP BY PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_MODL, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PMNT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_RGDT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_TYPE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ENGN, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLNO, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NOTE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_CDTE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_CLSE;


--
-- V_S_VHCL_REG  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_S_VHCL_REG
(DEPOT_ID, DEPOT_NAME, VHCL_ID, VHCL_NAME, OWNR_NAME, 
 VHCL_MODL, VHCL_PMNT, VHCL_RGDT, VHCL_TYPE, VHCL_ENGN, 
 VHCL_PLNO, VHCL_NOTE, VHCL_CDTE, VHCL_CLSE)
AS 
SELECT ALL 
PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_MODL, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PMNT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_RGDT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_TYPE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ENGN, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLNO, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NOTE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_CDTE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_CLSE 
FROM PM.PM_DEPOT, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_OWNR 
WHERE PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID= PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID 
AND PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID 
AND PM.PM_VHCL.TEXT='Sales' 
AND PM.PM_DEPOT.TEXT='Sales' 
AND PM.PM_OWNR.TEXT='Sales' 
GROUP BY PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_MODL, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PMNT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_RGDT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_TYPE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ENGN, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PLNO, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NOTE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_CDTE, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_CLSE;


--
-- V_VHCL_PAYMENT  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_PMNT (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_VHCL_PAYMENT
(PMNT_DATE, PMNT_AMNT, OWNR_ID, OWNR_NAME, VHCL_ID, 
 VHCL_NAME, DEPOT_NAME, CMP)
AS 
SELECT ALL PM.PM_PMNT.PMNT_DATE, PM.PM_PMNT.PMNT_AMNT, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID,
PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME,PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME,PM.PM_DEPOT.CMP
FROM PM.PM_OWNR, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_PMNT,PM.PM_DEPOT
WHERE PM.PM_VHCL.TEXT='Sales'
 AND  ((PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID)
 AND (PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID=PM.PM_PMNT.OWNR_ID)
  AND (PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID=PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID)) order by PMNT_DATE asc;


--
-- V_VHCL_PAYMENT_OLD  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_PMNT (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_VHCL_PAYMENT_OLD
(PMNT_DATE, PMNT_AMNT, OWNR_ID, OWNR_NAME, VHCL_ID, 
 VHCL_NAME, DEPOT_NAME)
AS 
SELECT ALL PM.PM_PMNT.PMNT_DATE, PM.PM_PMNT.PMNT_AMNT, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID,
PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME,PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME
FROM PM.PM_OWNR, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_PMNT,PM.PM_DEPOT
WHERE PM.PM_VHCL.TEXT='Sales'
 AND  ((PM.PM_OWNR.VHCL_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID)
 AND (PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID=PM.PM_PMNT.OWNR_ID)
  AND (PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID=PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID)) order by PMNT_DATE asc;


--
-- V_VHCL_REG  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   PM_DEPOT (Table)
--   PM_OWNR (Table)
--   PM_VHCL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.V_VHCL_REG
(DEPOT_ID, DEPOT_NAME, VHCL_ID, VHCL_NAME, OWNR_ID, 
 OWNR_NAME, VHCL_MODL, VHCL_PMNT, VHCL_RGDT, VHCL_TYPE)
AS 
SELECT ALL  PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_MODL, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PMNT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_RGDT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_TYPE
FROM PM.PM_DEPOT, PM.PM_VHCL, PM.PM_OWNR
WHERE (PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID= PM.PM_VHCL.DEPOT_ID) 
AND (PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID=PM.PM_VHCL.OWNR_ID) 
AND (PM.PM_VHCL.TEXT='Transport') 
AND (PM.PM_DEPOT.TEXT='Transport')
AND (PM.PM_OWNR.TEXT='Transport')
GROUP BY PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_ID, PM.PM_DEPOT.DEPOT_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_ID, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_NAME, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_ID, PM.PM_OWNR.OWNR_NAME, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_MODL, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_PMNT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_RGDT, PM.PM_VHCL.VHCL_TYPE;


--
-- XXE_LICENSE  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   C_COMPANY_GROUP (Table)
--   LIC_COMPANY (Table)
--   LIC_ISSUING_AUTHORITY (Table)
--   LIC_LICENSE (Table)
--   LIC_LICENSE_NAME (Table)
--   LIC_LOCATION (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.XXE_LICENSE
(CO_CODE, LICENSE_NAME, AUTHORITY_NAME, LICENSE_ID, EXPIRE_DATE, 
 REMARK, LICENSE_NUMBER, LICENSE_HOLDER_EMAIL, LICENSE_HOLDER_MOBILE, LPEND, 
 LEXPIRE, SHORT_NAME, LOCATION_NAME, COMPANY_NAME)
AS 
SELECT 
    NVL(CASE
               WHEN C.CO_CODE = 'MAL' THEN 'PBL'
               WHEN C.CO_CODE = 'BISCUIT' THEN 'BBL'
               ELSE C.CO_CODE
           END,'OTHER')
               CO_CODE,
           LN.LICENSE_NAME,
           AUTHORITY_NAME,
           --COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_LICENSES,
           L.LICENSE_ID,
           L.EXPIRE_DATE,
           --LN.REMARKS,
           L.REMARK,
           L.LICENSE_NUMBER,
           LICENSE_HOLDER_EMAIL,
           LICENSE_HOLDER_MOBILE,
           CASE
               WHEN     TO_CHAR (L.EXPIRE_DATE, 'YYYYMM') =
                        TO_CHAR (SYSDATE - 1, 'YYYYMM')
                    AND TRUNC (L.EXPIRE_DATE) >= TRUNC (SYSDATE - 1)
               THEN
                   1
               ELSE
                   0
           END
               LPEND,
           CASE
               WHEN TRUNC (L.EXPIRE_DATE) <= TRUNC (SYSDATE - 1) THEN 1
               ELSE 0
           END
               LEXPIRE,
               C.SHORT_NAME,
               LOCATION_NAME,
               COMPANY_NAME
      FROM PM.LIC_LICENSE  L
           LEFT JOIN PM.LIC_LICENSE_NAME LN
               ON L.LICENSE_NAME_ID = LN.LICENSE_NAME_ID
           LEFT JOIN PM.LIC_COMPANY C ON L.COMPANY_ID = C.COMPANY_ID
           INNER JOIN PM.C_COMPANY_GROUP CG
               ON C.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = CG.COMPANY_GROUP_ID
           INNER JOIN PM.LIC_ISSUING_AUTHORITY IA
               ON IA.ISSUING_AUTHORITY_ID = L.ISSUING_AUTHORITY_ID
               INNER JOIN PM.LIC_LOCATION LL
               ON LL.LIC_LOCATION_ID = L.LIC_LOCATION_ID
     WHERE     L.ACT = 1
           AND L.LICENSE_STATUS_ID = 1
           AND LN.LICENSE_TYPE_ID = 2
           --AND TO_CHAR(L.EXPIRE_DATE,'YYYYMM') = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE-1,'YYYYMM')
           --AND NVL(TRUNC(SYSDATE - L.EXPIRE_DATE), 0) <= 0
           AND C.COMPANY_GROUP_ID = 1
           AND C.CO_CODE IS NOT NULL
-- AND (TO_CHAR(L.EXPIRE_DATE,'YYYYMM') = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE-1,'YYYYMM') OR TRUNC(L.EXPIRE_DATE) <= TRUNC(SYSDATE-1))
;


--
-- DOC_SHDL_IDGEN  (Trigger) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   DOC_SHDL (Table)
--   DBMS_STANDARD (Package)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE TRIGGER PM.DOC_SHDL_IDGEN
 BEFORE INSERT ON PM.DOC_SHDL
 FOR EACH ROW
DECLARE
 V_NO NUMBER;
 BEGIN
IF INSERTING THEN
 SELECT NVL(MAX(OID),0) INTO V_NO FROM PM.DOC_SHDL;
 :NEW.OID:=LPAD(V_NO,10,'0')+1;
END IF;
END;
/

BEGIN
IF UPDATING THEN
:NEW.EDAT :=SYSDATE;
:NEW.EUSER := USER;
:NEW.UDT := TO_DATE(SYSDATE,'DD/MM/YY');
:NEW.VER := :OLD.VER + 1;
END IF;
END;
/

--
--  Dependencies: 
--   T_MKTG (Table)
--   T_MKTB (Table)
--   DBMS_STANDARD (Package)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--

UPDATE ON PM.T_MKTG FOR EACH ROW
BEGIN
IF UPDATING THEN
insert into PM.T_MKTB(OID,MKTG_NAME,MKTG_ADDS) values (:OLD.OID,:OLD.MKTG_NAME,:OLD.MKTG_ADDS);
END IF;
END;
/

--
-- SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL  (Scheduler Job) 
--
BEGIN
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.CREATE_JOB
    (
       job_name        => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
      ,start_date      => TO_TIMESTAMP_TZ('2025/08/02 17:11:48.125237 +06:00','yyyy/mm/dd hh24:mi:ss.ff tzh:tzm')
      ,repeat_interval => 'FREQ=DAILY;BYHOUR=08;BYMINUTE=45;BYSECOND=0;INTERVAL=1'
      ,end_date        => NULL
      ,job_class       => 'DEFAULT_JOB_CLASS'
      ,job_type        => 'STORED_PROCEDURE'
      ,job_action      => 'PM.INSERT_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
      ,comments        => NULL
    );
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE
    ( name      => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
     ,attribute => 'RESTARTABLE'
     ,value     => TRUE);
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE
    ( name      => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
     ,attribute => 'LOGGING_LEVEL'
     ,value     => SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.LOGGING_OFF);
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE_NULL
    ( name      => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
     ,attribute => 'MAX_FAILURES');
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE_NULL
    ( name      => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
     ,attribute => 'MAX_RUNS');
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE
    ( name      => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
     ,attribute => 'STOP_ON_WINDOW_CLOSE'
     ,value     => FALSE);
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE
    ( name      => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
     ,attribute => 'JOB_PRIORITY'
     ,value     => 3);
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE_NULL
    ( name      => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
     ,attribute => 'SCHEDULE_LIMIT');
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE
    ( name      => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL'
     ,attribute => 'AUTO_DROP'
     ,value     => FALSE);

  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.ENABLE
    (name                  => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL');
END;
/


--
-- SJOB_ADT_REPORT_STATUS  (Scheduler Job) 
--
BEGIN
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.CREATE_JOB
    (
       job_name        => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_REPORT_STATUS'
      ,start_date      => TO_TIMESTAMP_TZ('2025/01/19 11:45:30.289568 Asia/Dacca','yyyy/mm/dd hh24:mi:ss.ff tzr')
      ,repeat_interval => 'FREQ=DAILY;BYHOUR=08,16;BYMINUTE=01;BYSECOND=0;INTERVAL=1'
      ,end_date        => NULL
      ,job_class       => 'DEFAULT_JOB_CLASS'
      ,job_type        => 'STORED_PROCEDURE'
      ,job_action      => 'PM.PRO_ADT_REPORT_STATUS'
      ,comments        => NULL
    );
  SYS.DBMS_SCHEDULER.SET_ATTRIBUTE
    ( name      => 'PM.SJOB_ADT_REPORT_STATUS'
