--
-- AV_NST_CURSOR  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_MENUC (Table)
--   A_MENUP (Table)
--   A_USERS_MENUC (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.av_nst_cursor(
    vLOV IN VARCHAR2,
vUSER IN VARCHAR2,
    av_cur OUT sys_refcursor)
AS
BEGIN
  IF vLOV = 'Y' THEN
    OPEN av_cur FOR SELECT P.PMNU_TEXT, P.PMNU_NAME, P.PMNU_ICON, C.CMNU_TEXT,
C.CMNU_NAME, C.CMNU_ICON, C.CMNU_NOTE,
CASE
WHEN U.ACT = '1'
THEN 'T'
WHEN U.ACT = '0'
THEN 'F'
ELSE 'N'
END ISOK
FROM A_MENUC C INNER JOIN A_MENUP P ON C.CMNU_PMNU = P.PMNU_TEXT
LEFT OUTER JOIN A_USERS_MENUC U
ON C.CMNU_TEXT = U.CMNU_TEXT AND U.USER_TEXT ='78278'
WHERE C.CMNU_NOTE = 'KSA' AND C.ACT = 1
ORDER BY ISOK DESC, P.PMNU_TEXT ASC, C.CMNU_TEXT ASC;
  END IF;
END; 
/


--
-- INSERT_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   VW_ADT_FST_FLW_UP_DAY_BEFORE (View)
--   DBMS_OUTPUT (Synonym)
--   T_MAILOUT (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.INSERT_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL AS
BEGIN
    -- Loop through the data from VW_ADT_FST_FLW_UP_DAY_BEFORE
    FOR rec IN (
        SELECT MAIL_TO, MAIL_CC, MAIL_BCC, MAIL_SUBJECT, MAIL_BODY, REFNO
        FROM VW_ADT_FST_FLW_UP_DAY_BEFORE
    ) LOOP
        -- Insert data into T_MAILOUT@Mail_db_link
        INSERT INTO T_MAILOUT@Mail_db_link (
            MAIL_TO,
            MAIL_CC,
            MAIL_BCC,
            MAIL_SUBJT,
            MAIL_BODYHTML,
            MAIL_REFERENCE
        )
        VALUES (
            rec.MAIL_TO,
            'ga@prangroup.com;mis@prangroup.com;mis4@prangroup.com;mis29@mis.prangroup.com;mis34@mis.prangroup.com',
            rec.MAIL_BCC,
            rec.MAIL_SUBJECT,
            rec.MAIL_BODY,
            rec.REFNO
        );
    END LOOP;

    -- Commit the transaction to save changes
    COMMIT;

    -- Output success message
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Data successfully inserted into T_MAILOUT@Mail_db_link.');

EXCEPTION
    WHEN OTHERS THEN
        -- In case of an error, roll back the transaction
        ROLLBACK;
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Error occurred: ' || SQLERRM);
END INSERT_ADT_FST_FOLLOWUP_MAIL;
/


--
-- INSERT_LICENSE_MAIL_DATA  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   VW_LIC_EXPIRE_NOTIFY_MAIL (View)
--   DBMS_OUTPUT (Synonym)
--   T_MAILOUT (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.insert_license_mail_data AS
BEGIN
    -- Loop through the data from VW_LIC_EXPIRE_NOTIFY_MAIL
    FOR rec IN (
        SELECT MAIL_TO, MAIL_CC, MAIL_BCC, MAIL_SUBJECT, MAIL_BODY, REFNO
        FROM VW_LIC_EXPIRE_NOTIFY_MAIL
    ) LOOP
        -- Insert data into T_MAILOUT@Mail_db_link
        INSERT INTO T_MAILOUT@Mail_db_link (
            MAIL_TO, MAIL_CC, MAIL_BCC, MAIL_SUBJT, MAIL_BODYHTML, MAIL_REFERENCE
        )
        VALUES (
            rec.MAIL_TO,
            'md@prangroup.com;mis@prangroup.com;mis4@prangroup.com;mis29@mis.prangroup.com;mis34@mis.prangroup.com',
            rec.MAIL_BCC,
            rec.MAIL_SUBJECT,
            rec.MAIL_BODY,  -- Assuming MAIL_BODY goes to MAIL_BODYHTML
            rec.REFNO   -- Assuming TRN_DATE goes to MAIL_SENDTIME
        );
    END LOOP;

    -- Commit the transaction to save changes
    COMMIT;

    -- Output success message
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Data successfully inserted into T_MAILOUT@Mail_db_link.');
EXCEPTION
    WHEN OTHERS THEN
        -- In case of an error, roll back the transaction
        ROLLBACK;
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('Error: ' || SQLERRM);
END insert_license_mail_data;
/


--
-- LICENSE_EXPIRY_NOTIFICATIONS  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATE_COUNT (View)
--   DBMS_OUTPUT (Synonym)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.license_expiry_notifications IS
    -- Define variables for license information and email content
    v_license_id          VARCHAR2(50);
    v_license_number      VARCHAR2(50);
    v_license_holder_email VARCHAR2(100);
    v_remaining_days      NUMBER;
    v_issue_date          DATE;
    v_expire_date         DATE;
    v_subject             VARCHAR2(255);
    v_body                VARCHAR2(4000);
BEGIN
    -- Cursor to select records where the remaining days are 45, 30, or 7
    FOR rec IN (
        SELECT LICENSE_ID, LICENSE_NUMBER, LICENSE_HOLDER_EMAIL, REMAINING, ISSUE_DATE, EXPIRE_DATE
        FROM VW_LIC_EXPIRE_DATE_COUNT
        WHERE REMAINING IN (45, 30, 7)
    ) LOOP
        v_license_id := rec.LICENSE_ID;
        v_license_number := rec.LICENSE_NUMBER;
        v_license_holder_email := rec.LICENSE_HOLDER_EMAIL;
        v_remaining_days := rec.REMAINING;
        v_issue_date := rec.ISSUE_DATE;
        v_expire_date := rec.EXPIRE_DATE;

        -- Compose the email subject
        v_subject := 'License Expiry Notification for License ' || v_license_number;

        -- Compose the email body based on the remaining days
        v_body := 'Dear License Holder,<br><br>';
        v_body := v_body || 'This is a reminder that your license with number ' || v_license_number || ' will expire in ' || v_remaining_days || ' days.<br>';
        v_body := v_body || 'Issue Date: ' || TO_CHAR(v_issue_date, 'DD-Mon-YYYY') || '<br>';
        v_body := v_body || 'Expiration Date: ' || TO_CHAR(v_expire_date, 'DD-Mon-YYYY') || '<br><br>';
        v_body := v_body || 'Please take the necessary actions.<br><br>';
        v_body := v_body || 'Thanks and Regards,<br><b>Your Company Name</b><br>';
        v_body := v_body || '<b style="color: red; font-style: italic;">This is a system-generated email. Please do not reply to it.</b><br>';

        -- Use UTL_MAIL to send the email
        BEGIN
            UTL_MAIL.send(
                sender => 'BankGuarantee@Prangroup.com',  -- Sender email address
                recipients => v_license_holder_email,  -- Recipient email address
                subject => v_subject,  -- Subject of the email
                message => v_body,  -- Body of the email
                mime_type => 'text/html'  -- Email type (HTML format)
            );
        EXCEPTION
            WHEN OTHERS THEN
                -- Handle any errors that occur during sending the email
                DBMS_OUTPUT.put_line('Error sending email for License ID ' || v_license_id || ': ' || SQLERRM);
        END;
    END LOOP;
END license_expiry_notifications;
/


--
-- PRO_SUM_SIX  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_PLEA (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_SUM_SIX(USERID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
/*Police Case Data Summery Generator -KSA*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_T NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT SUM(PLEA_AMNT) INTO VALUE_A FROM A_PLEA WHERE PLEA_STATUS='N';
SELECT SUM(PLEA_AMNT) INTO VALUE_B FROM A_PLEA WHERE PLEA_STATUS='Y';
SELECT SUM(PLEA_AMNT) INTO VALUE_T FROM A_PLEA;
UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B,DATA_J=VALUE_T,UDU=USERID,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE)
WHERE SV_TABLE='PRO_SUM_SIX';
COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- USER_MENU_PERMISSION  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_MENUC (Table)
--   A_MENUP (Table)
--   A_USERS_MENUC (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.USER_MENU_PERMISSION(
vUSER IN VARCHAR2,
vLOV IN VARCHAR2,
av_cur OUT sys_refcursor)
AS
BEGIN
IF vLOV = 'Y' THEN
OPEN av_cur FOR SELECT P.PMNU_TEXT, P.PMNU_NAME, P.PMNU_ICON, C.CMNU_TEXT,
C.CMNU_NAME, C.CMNU_ICON, C.CMNU_NOTE,
CASE
WHEN U.ACT = '1'
THEN 'T'
WHEN U.ACT = '0'
THEN 'F'
ELSE 'N'
END ISOK
FROM A_MENUC C INNER JOIN A_MENUP P ON C.CMNU_PMNU = P.PMNU_TEXT
LEFT OUTER JOIN A_USERS_MENUC U
ON C.CMNU_TEXT = U.CMNU_TEXT AND U.USER_TEXT =vUSER
WHERE C.CMNU_NOTE = 'KSA' AND C.ACT = 1
ORDER BY ISOK DESC, P.PMNU_TEXT ASC, C.CMNU_TEXT ASC;
END IF;
END;

--
-- VW_PMENU_CMENU  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_MENUC (Table)
--   A_MENUP (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_PMENU_CMENU
(PMNU_TEXT, PMNU_NAME, CMNU_TEXT, CMNU_NAME, CMNU_ROLE, 
 CMNU_NOTE)
AS 
SELECT  p.pmnu_text, p.pmnu_name, c.cmnu_text, c.cmnu_name, c.cmnu_role, c.cmnu_note
       FROM a_menuc c JOIN a_menup p ON c.cmnu_pmnu = p.pmnu_text
                                        AND p.act = 1
      WHERE c.act = 1
   ORDER BY p.pmnu_text, c.cmnu_text;


--
-- VW_RPT_BILL_SUM  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_BILL (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_RPT_BILL_SUM
(BILH_ID, BILH_DATE, JOBH_ID, BILL_AMT)
AS 
select bh.bilh_id,bh.bilh_date,bh.JOBH_ID,sum(bh.bilh_ipay+bh.bilh_upay+bh.bilh_cpay)as BILL_AMT
from a_bill bh
group by bh.bilh_id,bh.bilh_date,bh.JOBH_ID;

--
-- PRO_SUM_EIGHT  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_BILL (Table)
--   A_INSPD (Table)
--   A_INSPH (Table)
--   A_JOBD (Table)
--   A_JOBH (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   DUAL (Synonym)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_SUM_EIGHT(USERID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
/* Last 6 Month Inspection JobCard Bill Data Generator*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_D NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT SUM(D.INSPD_IQTY*D.INSPD_IRATE) 
INTO VALUE_A 
FROM A_INSPD D INNER JOIN A_INSPH I ON D.INSPH_ID=I.INSPH_ID 
WHERE I.INSPH_DATE 
BETWEEN (SELECT TRUNC(TO_DATE(add_months(sysdate,-6),'DD-MM-YY')) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1) FROM DUAL) 
AND (SELECT ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1), 1) -1 FROM DUAL);
SELECT SUM(D.JOBD_IQTY*D.JOBD_IRATE),SUM(D.JOBD_JQTY*D.JOBD_JRATE) INTO VALUE_B, VALUE_C 
FROM A_JOBD D INNER JOIN A_JOBH I ON D.JOBH_ID=I.JOBH_ID 
WHERE I.JOBH_DATE 
BETWEEN (SELECT TRUNC(TO_DATE(add_months(sysdate,-6),'DD-MM-YY')) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1) FROM DUAL) 
AND (SELECT ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1), 1) -1 FROM DUAL);
SELECT SUM(BILH_IPAY+BILH_UPAY+BILH_CPAY) INTO VALUE_D 
FROM A_BILL B 
WHERE B.BILH_DATE 
BETWEEN (SELECT TRUNC(TO_DATE(add_months(sysdate,-6),'DD-MM-YY')) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1) FROM DUAL) 
AND (SELECT ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1), 1) -1 FROM DUAL);
UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C, DATA_D=VALUE_D,
UDU=USERID,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE)
WHERE SV_TABLE='PRO_SUM_EIGHT';
COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_SUM_SEVEN  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_BILL (Table)
--   A_INSPD (Table)
--   A_INSPH (Table)
--   A_JOBD (Table)
--   A_JOBH (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   DUAL (Synonym)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_SUM_SEVEN(USERID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
/* Inspection JobCard Bill Data Generator*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_D NUMBER(10);
VALUE_E NUMBER(10);
VALUE_F NUMBER(10);
VALUE_G NUMBER(10);
VALUE_H NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT NVL(SUM(D.INSPD_IQTY*D.INSPD_IRATE),0) 
INTO VALUE_A 
FROM A_INSPD D INNER JOIN A_INSPH I ON D.INSPH_ID=I.INSPH_ID 
WHERE I.INSPH_DATE 
BETWEEN (SELECT TRUNC(SYSDATE) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1) FROM DUAL) 
AND (SELECT ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1), 1) -1 FROM DUAL);
SELECT NVL(SUM(D.JOBD_IQTY*D.JOBD_IRATE),0),NVL(SUM(D.JOBD_JQTY*D.JOBD_JRATE),0) INTO VALUE_B, VALUE_C 
FROM A_JOBD D INNER JOIN A_JOBH I ON D.JOBH_ID=I.JOBH_ID 
WHERE I.JOBH_DATE 
BETWEEN (SELECT TRUNC(SYSDATE) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1) FROM DUAL) 
AND (SELECT ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1), 1) -1 FROM DUAL);
SELECT NVL(SUM(BILH_IPAY+BILH_UPAY+BILH_CPAY),0) INTO VALUE_D 
FROM A_BILL B 
WHERE B.BILH_DATE 
BETWEEN (SELECT TRUNC(SYSDATE) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1) FROM DUAL) 
AND (SELECT ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE) - (TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DD')) - 1), 1) -1 FROM DUAL);
SELECT NVL(SUM(D.INSPD_IQTY*D.INSPD_IRATE),0) 
INTO VALUE_E 
FROM A_INSPD D INNER JOIN A_INSPH I ON D.INSPH_ID=I.INSPH_ID 
WHERE I.INSPH_DATE 
BETWEEN (SELECT TO_DATE(LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,-2))+1,'DD-MM-YY') AS FIRST_DAY FROM DUAL) 
AND (SELECT TO_DATE(LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,-1)),'DD-MM-YY') AS LAST_DAY FROM DUAL);
SELECT NVL(SUM(D.JOBD_IQTY*D.JOBD_IRATE),0),NVL(SUM(D.JOBD_JQTY*D.JOBD_JRATE),0) INTO VALUE_F, VALUE_G 
FROM A_JOBD D INNER JOIN A_JOBH I ON D.JOBH_ID=I.JOBH_ID 
WHERE I.JOBH_DATE 
BETWEEN (SELECT TO_DATE(LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,-2))+1,'DD-MM-YY') AS FIRST_DAY FROM DUAL) 
AND (SELECT TO_DATE(LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,-1)),'DD-MM-YY') AS LAST_DAY FROM DUAL);
SELECT NVL(SUM(BILH_IPAY+BILH_UPAY+BILH_CPAY),0) INTO VALUE_H 
FROM A_BILL B 
WHERE B.BILH_DATE 
BETWEEN (SELECT TO_DATE(LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,-2))+1,'DD-MM-YY') AS FIRST_DAY FROM DUAL) 
AND (SELECT TO_DATE(LAST_DAY(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,-1)),'DD-MM-YY') AS LAST_DAY FROM DUAL);
UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C, DATA_D=VALUE_D,
DATA_E=VALUE_E, DATA_F=VALUE_F, DATA_G=VALUE_G, DATA_H=VALUE_H,
UDU=USERID,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE)
WHERE SV_TABLE='PRO_SUM_SEVEN';
COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_SUM_THREE  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_BILL (Table)
--   A_INSPD (Table)
--   A_JOBD (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_SUM_THREE(USERID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
/* Inspection JobCard Bill Data Generator*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_D NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT SUM(INSPD_IQTY*INSPD_IRATE) INTO VALUE_A  FROM A_INSPD;
SELECT SUM(JOBD_IQTY*JOBD_IRATE),SUM(JOBD_JQTY*JOBD_JRATE) INTO VALUE_B, VALUE_C FROM A_JOBD;
SELECT SUM(BILH_IPAY+BILH_UPAY+BILH_CPAY) INTO VALUE_D FROM A_BILL;
UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C, DATA_D=VALUE_D,UDU=USERID ,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE)
WHERE SV_TABLE='PRO_SUM_THREE';
COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- VW_DMB_NOTIF  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AUTH (Table)
--   A_BUSINESS (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_DMB_REMINDER (Table)
--   A_DOCYPE (Table)
--   A_GROUP (Table)
--   A_PMTYPE (Table)
--   A_RNTYPE (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_DMB_NOTIF
(REMIND_ID, LIC_ID, ASSET, DOC_ID, DOC_NAME, 
 BUSINESS_ID, BUSINESS_NAME, DEPARTMENT_ID, DEPARTMENT_NAME, GROUP_ID, 
 GROUP_NAME, AUTH_ID, AUTH_NAME, RE_ID, RNT_NAME, 
 RPLACE, RVALUE, RUSER, RCONTACT, C_DATE, 
 N_DATE, REMAINING, REMINDER_NOTE, PT_ID, PMT_NAME, 
 NOTE, REMIND, COMPLETED)
AS 
SELECT   R.REMIND_ID,
         R.LIC_ID,
         r.ASSET,
         R.DOC_ID,
         DC.DOC_NAME,
         R.BUSINESS_ID,
         B.BUSINESS_NAME,
         R.DEPARTMENT_ID,
         D.DEPARTMENT_NAME,
         R.GROUP_ID,
         GR.GROUP_NAME,
         R.AUTH_ID,
         AU.AUTH_NAME,
         R.RE_ID,
         RE.RNT_NAME,
         R.RPLACE,
         R.RVALUE,
         R.RUSER,
         R.RCONTACT,
         R.C_DATE,
         R.N_DATE,
           TO_DATE (R.N_DATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
         - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
         + 1
            AS remaining,
         CASE
            WHEN (TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (N_DATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')) > 0
            THEN
               'Expired'
            ELSE
               'Running'
         END
            REMINDER_NOTE,
         R.PT_ID,
         P.PMT_NAME,
         R.NOTE,
         R.REMIND,
         R.COMPLETED
  FROM                        PM.A_DMB_REMINDER R
                           INNER JOIN
                              PM.A_BUSINESS B
                           ON R.BUSINESS_ID = B.BUSINESS_ID AND B.ACT = 1
                        INNER JOIN
                           PM.A_DEPARTMENT D
                        ON R.DEPARTMENT_ID = D.DEPARTMENT_ID AND D.ACT = 1
                     INNER JOIN
                        PM.A_GROUP GR
                     ON R.GROUP_ID = GR.GROUP_ID AND GR.ACT = 1
                  INNER JOIN
                     PM.A_AUTH AU
                  ON R.AUTH_ID = AU.AUTH_TEXT AND AU.ACT = 1
               INNER JOIN
                  PM.A_PMTYPE P
               ON R.PT_ID = P.PMT_TEXT AND P.ACT = 1
            INNER JOIN
               PM.A_DOCYPE DC
            ON R.DOC_ID = DC.DOC_TEXT AND DC.ACT = 1
         INNER JOIN
            PM.A_RNTYPE RE
         ON R.RE_ID = RE.RNT_TEXT and RE.ACT = 1
 WHERE   R.COMPLETED = 'N';


--
-- VW_IQAMA_NOTIF_V1  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_BUSINESS (Table)
--   A_COMPANY (Table)
--   A_COUNTRY (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_IQAMA_NOTIF_V1
(OWNR_ID, OWNR_TEXT, OWNR_USER, OWNR_NAME, AAJKEY, 
 OWNR_NDATE, ELAPSED, REMIND, BUSINESS_ID, OWNR_AREA, 
 GROUP_NAME, REMINDER_NOTE, NOTIF_MESSAGES, MAIL_SEND, UPDT)
AS 
SELECT o.ownr_id, o.ownr_text, o.ownr_user, o.ownr_name,
          TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy') aajkey, o.ownr_ndate,
            TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          + 1 AS elapsed,
          o.remind, b.business_id, o.ownr_area, 'GR0001' AS group_name,
          CASE
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 31 AND 91
                THEN 'First_Reminder'
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 1 AND 31
                THEN 'Expired_Soon'
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) < 1
                THEN 'Expired'
             ELSE 'Running'
          END reminder_note,
          CASE
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 31 AND 91
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN o.remind = 0
                            THEN 'First Reminder Mail Need To Send'
                         WHEN o.remind > 1
                            THEN 'First Reminder But All Mail Sent'
                         ELSE 'First Reminder Mail Already Sent'
                      END
                     )
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 1 AND 31
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN o.remind = 0
                            THEN 'First Reminder Mail Already Sent But Need Final Mail'
                         WHEN o.remind = 1
                            THEN 'Final Reminder Mail Need To Send'
                         ELSE 'Final Reminder All Mail Sent'
                      END
                     )
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) < 1
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN o.remind = 0
                            THEN 'Expired But Single Mail Need To Send'
                         ELSE 'Expired Reminder Mail Sent'
                      END
                     )
             WHEN o.remind <> 0
                THEN 'Vacation But 1st Mail Sent'
             ELSE 'In Vacation'
          END notif_messages,
          CASE
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 31 AND 91
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN o.remind = 0
                            THEN '1'
                         ELSE '0'
                      END)
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 1 AND 31
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN o.remind = 0
                            THEN '1'
                         WHEN o.remind = 1
                            THEN '1'
                         ELSE '0'
                      END
                     )
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) < 1
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN o.remind = 0
                            THEN '1'
                         ELSE '0'
                      END)
             ELSE '0'
          END mail_send,
          '1' updt
     FROM a_ownr o INNER JOIN a_department d ON o.ownr_dept = d.department_id
          INNER JOIN a_business b ON d.department_business = b.business_id
          INNER JOIN a_company c ON b.business_company = c.company_id
          INNER JOIN a_country a
          ON c.company_country = a.country_id AND a.country_id = 'KSA'
    WHERE o.ownr_type = 'Staff' AND o.ownr_resign = 'N' AND o.act = 1;


--
-- VW_IQAMA_NOTIF_V2  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_BUSINESS (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_IQAMA_NOTIF_V2
(OWNR_ID, OWNR_TEXT, OWNR_USER, OWNR_NAME, OWNR_NOTE, 
 AAJ_DATE, EXP_DATE, REMIND, BUSINESS_ID, GROUP_NAME, 
 ELAPSED, MESSAGES, MESSAGE_NOTE, MAIL_SEND, MAIL_SUBJECT, 
 MAIL_BODY, UPDT)
AS 
SELECT o.ownr_id, o.ownr_text, o.ownr_user, o.ownr_name, o.ownr_note,
          TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd/MM/yyyy') aaj_date,
          TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd/MM/yyyy') exp_date, o.remind,
          b.business_id, 'GR0001' group_name,
            TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          + 1 AS elapsed,
          CASE
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 31 AND 91
                THEN 'First_Reminder'
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 1 AND 31
                THEN 'Final_Reminder'
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) < 1
                THEN 'Expired'
             ELSE 'Running'
          END messages,
          CASE
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 31 AND 91
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN o.remind = 0
                          THEN 'Need_First_Mail'
                       ELSE 'First_Mail_Sent'
                    END
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 1 AND 31
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN o.remind < 1
                          THEN 'Need_First_Mail'
                       WHEN o.remind < 2
                          THEN 'Need_Final_Mail'
                       ELSE 'Final_Mail_Sent'
                    END
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) < 1
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN o.remind < 1
                          THEN 'Need_Single_Mail'
                       ELSE 'Expired'
                    END
             ELSE 'In Vacation'
          END message_note,
          CASE
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 31 AND 91
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN o.remind = 0
                          THEN '1'
                       ELSE '0'
                    END
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) BETWEEN 1 AND 31
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN o.remind < 1
                          THEN '1'
                       WHEN o.remind < 2
                          THEN '1'
                       ELSE '0'
                    END
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1
                  ) < 1
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN o.remind < 1
                          THEN '1'
                       ELSE '0'
                    END
             ELSE '0'
          END mail_send,
          'Iqama-' || o.ownr_name || '-' || o.ownr_user AS mail_subject,
          '<p>Dear Concern,</p><p>Your document will expire soon, Please take necessary action-</p><p>Staff Id: <strong>{0}</strong></p><p>Staff Name: <strong>{1}</strong> </p><p>Note: <strong>{2}</strong> </p><p>Iqama No: <strong>{3}</strong> </p><p>Notification Messages: <strong>{4}</strong> </p><p>Mail Date: <strong>{5}</strong></p><p>Expire Date: <strong>{6}</strong></p><p>Remining Day: <strong>{7}</strong> Days</p><p>Thank you</p><hr/><p>This is a system generated email, do not reply to this email id.</p>'
                                                                 AS mail_body,
          CASE
             WHEN   TO_DATE (o.ownr_ndate, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 < 92
                THEN 'update a_ownr x set x.REMIND=x.REMIND+1 where x.OWNR_ID'
             ELSE '0'
          END updt
     FROM a_ownr o INNER JOIN a_department d ON o.ownr_dept = d.department_id
          INNER JOIN a_business b ON d.department_business = b.business_id
    WHERE o.act = 1 AND o.ownr_type = 'Staff' AND o.ownr_resign = 'N';


--
-- VW_RPT_INSP_SUM  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_INSPD (Table)
--   A_INSPH (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_RPT_INSP_SUM
(INSPH_ASET, INSPH_DATE, INSPH_ID, INSPH_OWNR, INSPH_AREA, 
 INSPH_DEPARTMENT, INSP_AMT)
AS 
SELECT   ih.insph_aset, insph_date, ih.insph_id,IH.INSPH_OWNR,ih.INSPH_Area,ih.INSPH_DEPARTMENT,
            SUM (ind.inspd_iqty * ind.inspd_irate) AS insp_amt
       FROM a_insph ih INNER JOIN a_inspd ind ON ih.insph_id = ind.insph_id
   GROUP BY ih.insph_aset, ih.insph_id, insph_date,IH.INSPH_OWNR,ih.INSPH_Area,ih.INSPH_DEPARTMENT
   ORDER BY ih.insph_aset;

            INITIAL          64K
            NEXT             1M
            MINEXTENTS       1
            MAXEXTENTS       UNLIMITED
            PCTINCREASE      0
            BUFFER_POOL      DEFAULT
           );

--
-- PRO_SUM_FOUR  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_SUM_FOUR(USERID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
/*Asset Data Summery Generator -KSA*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_T NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_A  FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0001' AND ACT='1' AND ASET_USED='1';
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_B FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0001' AND ACT='1' AND ASET_USED='0';
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_C  FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0001' AND ACT='0' AND ASET_ID <>'0';
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_T  FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0001' AND ASET_ID <>'0';
UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C,DATA_J=VALUE_T,UDU=USERID,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE) 
WHERE SV_TABLE='PRO_SUM_FOUR';
COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- PRO_SUM_ONE  (Procedure) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_SUMMARY_VIEW (Table)
--   STANDARD (Package)
--   SYS_STUB_FOR_PURITY_ANALYSIS (Package)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE PM.PRO_SUM_ONE(USERID IN VARCHAR2)
IS
/*Asset Data Summery Generator -ADMIN*/
VALUE_A NUMBER(10);
VALUE_B NUMBER(10);
VALUE_C NUMBER(10);
VALUE_D NUMBER(10);
VALUE_E NUMBER(10);
VALUE_T NUMBER(10);
BEGIN
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_A  FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0001' AND ASET_ID <>'0';
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_B FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0002' AND ASET_ID <>'0';
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_C  FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0003' AND ASET_ID <>'0';
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_D  FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0004' AND ASET_ID <>'0';
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_E  FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0005' AND ASET_ID <>'0';
SELECT COUNT(ASET_ID) INTO VALUE_T  FROM A_ASET_DATA WHERE ASET_GROUP='GR0005' AND ASET_ID <>'0';
UPDATE A_SUMMARY_VIEW SET DATA_A=VALUE_A, DATA_B=VALUE_B, DATA_C=VALUE_C, DATA_D=VALUE_D, DATA_E=VALUE_E,DATA_J=VALUE_T,UDU=USERID,UDT=TO_DATE(SYSDATE)
WHERE SV_TABLE='PRO_SUM_ONE';
COMMIT;
END; 
/


--
-- VW_ASET_BY_USERS  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_DISTRICT (Table)
--   A_THANA (Table)
--   A_USERS_AREA (Table)
--   A_USERS_BUS (Table)
--   A_USERS_COUNTRY (Table)
--   A_USERS_GROUP (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_ASET_BY_USERS
(ASET_ID, ASET_TAG, ASET_CODE_NAME, ASET_DEPARTMENT, ASET_AREA, 
 ASET_GROUP, ASET_USED, ACT, UG_USER)
AS 
select aset_id, aset_tag, aset_code_name, aset_department, aset_area,
aset_group, aset_used, a.act,g.ug_user
from a_aset_data a join a_users_group g
on a.aset_group = g.ug_group and  g.act = 1
join a_department d on a.aset_department = d.department_id
join a_users_bus ub
on d.department_business = ub.ub_business
and ub.act = 1
and ub.ub_user = g.ug_user
where a.aset_area in (
select f.area_id
from a_users_country c join a_district d
on c.country_id = d.district_country
join a_thana e on d.district_id = e.thana_district
join a_area f on e.thana_id = f.area_thana
where c.user_text = g.ug_user and c.act = 1
union all
select a.ua_area
from a_users_area a
where ua_user = g.ug_user and a.act = 1);


--
-- VW_BNDL_INSP  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_INSPD (Table)
--   A_INSPH (Table)
--   A_ITEM_LIST (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_BNDL_INSP
(INSPH_ASET, ASET_CODE_NAME, INSPH_OWNR, OWNR_USER, INSPH_DEPARTMENT, 
 DEPARTMENT_NAME, DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, INSPH_AREA, AREA_NAME, INSPH_IUSER, 
 INSPH_DATE, MILEAGE, INSPH_TYPE, INSPH_NOTE, INSPH_ID, 
 INSPD_LINE, INSPD_ITEM, ITEM_NAME, INSPD_IQTY, INSPD_IRATE, 
 INSPD_NOTE, INSPH_APP, INSPH_JOB)
AS 
SELECT i.insph_aset, a.aset_code_name, i.insph_ownr,o.ownr_user, i.insph_department,
          d.department_name, d.department_business, i.insph_area, r.area_name,
          i.insph_iuser, i.insph_date, i.mileage, i.insph_type, i.insph_note,
          b.insph_id, b.inspd_line, b.inspd_item, l.item_name, b.inspd_iqty,
          b.inspd_irate, b.inspd_note,i.insph_app, i.insph_job
     FROM a_insph i INNER JOIN a_aset_data a ON i.insph_aset = a.aset_id
          INNER JOIN a_ownr o ON i.insph_ownr = o.ownr_id
          INNER JOIN a_department d ON i.insph_department = d.department_id
          INNER JOIN a_area r ON i.insph_area = r.area_id
          INNER JOIN a_inspd b ON i.insph_id = b.insph_id
                             AND b.inspd_state = 'B'
          INNER JOIN a_item_list l ON b.inspd_item = l.item_id
    WHERE  i.act = 1;


--
-- VW_BNDL_JOBC  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_ITEM_LIST (Table)
--   A_JOBD (Table)
--   A_JOBH (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_BNDL_JOBC
(JOBH_ASET, ASET_CODE_NAME, JOBH_OWNR, OWNR_USER, JOBH_DEPARTMENT, 
 DEPARTMENT_NAME, DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, JOBH_AREA, AREA_NAME, JOBH_VENDOR, 
 JOBH_DATE, JOBH_MILEAGE, JOBH_TYPE, JOBH_NOTE, JOBH_ID, 
 JOBD_LINE, JOBD_ITEM, ITEM_NAME, JOBD_IQTY, JOBD_IRATE, 
 JOBD_NOTE, JOBH_APP, JOBH_BILL)
AS 
SELECT I.JOBH_ASET, A.ASET_CODE_NAME, I.JOBH_OWNR, O.OWNR_USER,
I.JOBH_DEPARTMENT, D.DEPARTMENT_NAME, D.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS,
I.JOBH_AREA, R.AREA_NAME, I.JOBH_VENDOR, I.JOBH_DATE, I.JOBH_MILEAGE,
I.JOBH_TYPE, I.JOBH_NOTE, B.JOBH_ID, B.JOBD_LINE, B.JOBD_ITEM,
L.ITEM_NAME, B.JOBD_IQTY, B.JOBD_IRATE, B.JOBD_NOTE, I.JOBH_APP,
I.JOBH_BILL
FROM A_JOBH I 
INNER JOIN A_ASET_DATA A ON I.JOBH_ASET = A.ASET_ID
INNER JOIN A_OWNR O ON I.JOBH_OWNR = O.OWNR_ID
INNER JOIN A_DEPARTMENT D ON I.JOBH_DEPARTMENT = D.DEPARTMENT_ID
INNER JOIN A_AREA R ON I.JOBH_AREA = R.AREA_ID
INNER JOIN A_JOBD B ON I.JOBH_ID = B.JOBH_ID AND B.JOBD_STATE = 'B'
INNER JOIN A_ITEM_LIST L ON B.JOBD_ITEM = L.ITEM_ID
WHERE I.ACT = 1;


--
-- VW_DATE_NOTIF  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_ITEM_LIST (Table)
--   A_NOTIF_SET (Table)
--   A_REMINDER (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_DATE_NOTIF
(REMIND_ID, ASET_ID, ASET_TAG, ITEM_ID, ITEM_NAME, 
 C_DATE, N_DATE, ELAPSED, NOTE, REMIND, 
 NOTIF_FIRST, NOTIF_FINAL, REMINDER_NOTE, NOTIF_TYPE, COMPLETED)
AS 
SELECT r.remind_id,
           r.aset_id,
           a.aset_tag,
           r.item_id,
           i.item_name,
           r.c_date,
           r.n_date,
             TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
           - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
           + 1    AS elapsed,
           r.note,
           r.remind,
           s.notif_first,
           s.notif_final,
           CASE
               WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                    - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                    + 1 >
                    s.notif_first
               THEN
                   'Running'
               WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                    - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                    + 1 >
                    s.notif_final
               THEN
                   'First_Reminder'
               WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                    - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                    + 1 >
                    1
               THEN
                   'Expired_Soon'
               ELSE
                   'Expired'
           END    reminder_note,
           s.notif_type,
           r.completed
      FROM a_reminder  r
           INNER JOIN a_aset_data a ON r.aset_id = a.aset_id
           INNER JOIN a_item_list i ON r.item_id = i.item_id
           INNER JOIN a_notif_set s ON i.item_notif_set = s.notif_id
     WHERE r.completed = 'N' AND a.act = '1';


--
-- VW_INSURANCE_S1  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_INSUH (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_INSURANCE_S1
(INSU_ID, ASET_NAME_CODE, ASET_GROUP, DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, ASET_AREA, 
 INSU_POLICY, INSU_PREMIUM, INSU_COMP, INSU_COMP_ADDRESS, INSU_NOTE, 
 INSU_REG_START, INSU_REG_END, REMIND, DAYS)
AS 
SELECT I.INSU_ID, A.ASET_CODE_NAME || '(' || A.ASET_TAG || ')' ASET_NAME_CODE,
          A.ASET_GROUP, D.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, A.ASET_AREA, I.INSU_POLICY,
          I.INSU_PREMIUM, I.INSU_COMP, I.INSU_COMP_ADDRESS, I.INSU_NOTE,
          I.INSU_REG_START, I.INSU_REG_END, I.REMIND,
            TO_DATE (I.INSU_REG_END, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          + 1 AS DAYS
     FROM A_INSUH I INNER JOIN A_ASET_DATA A ON I.INSU_ASET = A.ASET_ID
          INNER JOIN A_DEPARTMENT D ON A.ASET_DEPARTMENT = D.DEPARTMENT_ID
    WHERE I.ACT = 1;


--
-- VW_INSURANCE_S2  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   VW_INSURANCE_S1 (View)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_INSURANCE_S2
(INSU_ID, ASET_NAME_CODE, INSURANCE, COMPANY, INSU_NOTE, 
 MESSAGES, NEED_MAIL, INSU_REG_START, INSU_REG_END, DAYS, 
 MAIL_SUBJECT, MAIL_BODY, UPDT, ASET_GROUP, DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS)
AS 
SELECT s.insu_id, s.aset_name_code,
          s.insu_policy || '~' || s.insu_premium insurance,
          s.insu_comp || ',' || s.insu_comp_address company, s.insu_note,
          CASE
             WHEN s.days BETWEEN 32 AND 92
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 1
                          THEN 'This is First Mail'
                       ELSE 'First Reminder'
                    END
             WHEN s.days BETWEEN 1 AND 31
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 2
                          THEN 'This is Final Mail'
                       ELSE 'Final Reminder'
                    END
             WHEN s.days < 0
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 1
                          THEN 'This is First and Final Mail'
                       ELSE 'Expired'
                    END
             ELSE 'Unknown'
          END messages,
          CASE
             WHEN s.days BETWEEN 31 AND 92
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 1
                          THEN '1'
                       ELSE '0'
                    END
             WHEN s.days BETWEEN 1 AND 31
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 2
                          THEN '1'
                       ELSE '0'
                    END
             WHEN s.days < 0
                THEN CASE
                       WHEN remind < 1
                          THEN '1'
                       ELSE '0'
                    END
             ELSE '0'
          END need_mail,
          s.insu_reg_start, s.insu_reg_end, s.days,
          'Insurance-' || s.aset_name_code mail_subject,
          '<p>Dear Concern,</p><p>Your Asset Insurance will expire soon, Please take necessary action-</p><p>Asset Name: <strong>{0}</strong></p><p>Policy No - Premium: <strong>{1}</strong> </p><p>Company: <strong>{2}</strong> </p><p>Note: <strong>{3}</strong> </p><p>Notification Messages: <strong>{4}</strong> </p><p>Reg Date: <strong>{5}</strong></p><p>Expire Date: <strong>{6}</strong></p><p>Remining Day: <strong>{7}</strong> Days</p><p>Thank you</p><hr/><p>This is a system generated email, do not reply to this email id.</p>'
                                                                 AS mail_body,
          'UPDATE A_INSUH X SET X.REMIND=X.REMIND+1 WHERE X.INSU_ID' AS updt,
          s.aset_group, s.department_business
     FROM vw_insurance_s1 s
    WHERE s.days <= 92;


--
-- VW_NOTIF_MAIL  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_ITEM_LIST (Table)
--   A_NOTIF_SET (Table)
--   A_REMINDER (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_NOTIF_MAIL
(REMIND_ID, ASET_ID, ASET_TAG, ASET_CODE_NAME, ASET_DEPARTMENT, 
 DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, ASET_GROUP, ITEM_ID, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_NOTIF_SET, 
 NOTIF_TYPE, NOTIF_REMIND, NOTIF_FIRST, NOTIF_FINAL, C_DATE, 
 N_DATE, REMIND, NOTE, COMPLETED, ELAPSED, 
 REMINDER_NOTE, NOTIF_MESSAGES, MAIL_SEND, MAIL_SUBJECT, MAIL_BODY, 
 UPDT)
AS 
SELECT r.remind_id, r.aset_id, a.aset_tag, a.aset_code_name,
          a.aset_department, d.department_business, a.aset_group, r.item_id,
          i.item_name, i.item_notif_set, s.notif_type, s.notif_remind,
          s.notif_first, s.notif_final, r.c_date, r.n_date, r.remind, r.note,
          r.completed,
            TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          + 1 AS elapsed,
          CASE
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > s.notif_first
                THEN 'Running'
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1 > s.notif_final
                  )
             AND (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 < s.notif_first
                 )
                THEN 'First_Reminder'
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > 1
                THEN 'Expired_Soon'
             ELSE 'Expired'
          END reminder_note,
          CASE
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > s.notif_first
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind <> 0
                            THEN 'Vacation But 1st Mail Sent'
                         ELSE 'In Vacation'
                      END
                     )
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1 > s.notif_final
                  )
             AND (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 < s.notif_first
                 )
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind = 0
                            THEN 'First Reminder Mail Need To Send'
                         WHEN r.remind = 1
                            THEN 'First Reminder Mail Already Sent'
                         ELSE 'First Reminder But All Mail Sent'
                      END
                     )
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > 1
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind = 0
                            THEN 'Final Reminder Mail Need To Send'
                         WHEN r.remind = 1
                            THEN 'First Reminder Mail Already Sent But Need Final Mail'
                         --CHECK FIRST REMINDER
                      WHEN r.remind = 2
                            THEN 'Final Reminder Mail Already Sent'
                         ELSE 'Final Reminder All Mail Sent'
                      END
                     )
             ELSE (CASE
                      WHEN r.remind = 0
                         THEN 'Expired But Single Mail Need To Send'
                      ELSE 'Expired Reminder Mail Sent'
                   END
                  )
          END notif_messages,
          CASE
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > s.notif_first
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind <> 0
                            THEN '0'
                         ELSE '0'
                      END)
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1 > s.notif_final
                  )
             AND (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 < s.notif_first
                 )
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind = 0
                            THEN '1'
                         ELSE '0'
                      END)
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > 1
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind = 0
                            THEN '1'
                         WHEN r.remind = 1
                            THEN '1'                    --CHECK FIRST REMINDER
                         ELSE '0'
                      END
                     )
             ELSE (CASE
                      WHEN r.remind = 0
                         THEN '1'
                      ELSE '0'
                   END)
          END mail_send,
          'Reminder-' || i.item_name || '-'
          || a.aset_code_name AS mail_subject,
          '<p>Dear Concern,</p><p>Your Asset document will expire soon, Please take necessary action-</p><p>Aset Tag: <strong>{0}</strong></p><p>Aset Name: <strong>{1}</strong> </p><p>Note: <strong>{2}</strong> </p><p>Notifcation Name: <strong>{3}</strong> </p><p>Notification Messages: <strong>{4}</strong> </p><p>Issue Date: <strong>{5}</strong></p><p>Renew Date: <strong>{6}</strong></p><p>Remining Day: <strong>{7}</strong> Days</p><p>Thank you</p><hr/><p>This is a system generated email, do not reply to this email id.</p>'
                                                                    mail_body,
          'update a_reminder set REMIND=REMIND+1 where remind_id' updt
     FROM a_reminder r INNER JOIN a_aset_data a
          ON r.aset_id = a.aset_id AND a.act = 1
          INNER JOIN a_department d ON a.aset_department = d.department_id
          INNER JOIN a_item_list i ON r.item_id = i.item_id
          INNER JOIN a_notif_set s
          ON i.item_notif_set = s.notif_id AND s.notif_type = 'EMAIL'
    WHERE r.completed = 'N' AND (r.c_state IS NULL AND r.n_state IS NULL);


--
-- VW_NOTIF_MAIL_METER  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_ITEM_LIST (Table)
--   A_NOTIF_SET (Table)
--   A_REMINDER (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_NOTIF_MAIL_METER
(REMIND_ID, ASET_ID, ASET_TAG, ASET_CODE_NAME, ASET_DEPARTMENT, 
 DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, ASET_GROUP, ITEM_ID, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_NOTIF_SET, 
 NOTIF_TYPE, NOTIF_REMIND, NOTIF_FIRST, NOTIF_FINAL, C_DATE, 
 N_DATE, REMIND, NOTE, COMPLETED, ELAPSED, 
 REMINDER_NOTE, NOTIF_MESSAGES, MAIL_SEND, MAIL_SUBJECT, MAIL_BODY, 
 UPDT)
AS 
SELECT r.remind_id, r.aset_id, a.aset_tag, a.aset_code_name,
          a.aset_department, d.department_business, a.aset_group, r.item_id,
          i.item_name, i.item_notif_set, s.notif_type, s.notif_remind,
          s.notif_first, s.notif_final, r.c_date, r.n_date, r.remind, r.note,
          r.completed,
            TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
          + 1 AS elapsed,
          CASE
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > s.notif_first
                THEN 'Running'
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1 > s.notif_final
                  )
             AND (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 < s.notif_first
                 )
                THEN 'First_Reminder'
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > 1
                THEN 'Expired_Soon'
             ELSE 'Expired'
          END reminder_note,
          CASE
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > s.notif_first
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind <> 0
                            THEN 'Vacation But 1st Mail Sent'
                         ELSE 'In Vacation'
                      END
                     )
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1 > s.notif_final
                  )
             AND (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 < s.notif_first
                 )
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind = 0
                            THEN 'First Reminder Mail Need To Send'
                         WHEN r.remind = 1
                            THEN 'First Reminder Mail Already Sent'
                         ELSE 'First Reminder But All Mail Sent'
                      END
                     )
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > 1
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind = 0
                            THEN 'Final Reminder Mail Need To Send'
                         WHEN r.remind = 1
                            THEN 'First Reminder Mail Already Sent But Need Final Mail'
                         --CHECK FIRST REMINDER
                      WHEN r.remind = 2
                            THEN 'Final Reminder Mail Already Sent'
                         ELSE 'Final Reminder All Mail Sent'
                      END
                     )
             ELSE (CASE
                      WHEN r.remind = 0
                         THEN 'Expired But Single Mail Need To Send'
                      ELSE 'Expired Reminder Mail Sent'
                   END
                  )
          END notif_messages,
          CASE
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > s.notif_first
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind <> 0
                            THEN '0'
                         ELSE '0'
                      END)
             WHEN (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                   + 1 > s.notif_final
                  )
             AND (  TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 < s.notif_first
                 )
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind = 0
                            THEN '1'
                         ELSE '0'
                      END)
             WHEN   TO_DATE (r.n_date, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  - TO_DATE (SYSDATE, 'dd-MM-yyyy')
                  + 1 > 1
                THEN (CASE
                         WHEN r.remind = 0
                            THEN '1'
                         WHEN r.remind = 1
                            THEN '1'                    --CHECK FIRST REMINDER
                         ELSE '0'
                      END
                     )
             ELSE (CASE
                      WHEN r.remind = 0
                         THEN '1'
                      ELSE '0'
                   END)
          END mail_send,
          'Reminder-' || i.item_name || '-'
          || a.aset_code_name AS mail_subject,
          '<p>Dear Concern,</p><p>Your Asset document will expire soon, Please take necessary action-</p><p>Aset Tag: <strong>{0}</strong></p><p>Aset Name: <strong>{1}</strong> </p><p>Note: <strong>{2}</strong> </p><p>Notifcation Name: <strong>{3}</strong> </p><p>Notification Messages: <strong>{4}</strong> </p><p>Issue Date: <strong>{5}</strong></p><p>Renew Date: <strong>{6}</strong></p><p>Remining Day: <strong>{7}</strong> Days</p><p>Thank you</p><hr/><p>This is a system generated email, do not reply to this email id.</p>'
                                                                    mail_body,
          'update a_reminder set REMIND=REMIND+1 where remind_id=' updt
     FROM a_reminder r INNER JOIN a_aset_data a
          ON r.aset_id = a.aset_id AND a.act = 1
          INNER JOIN a_department d ON a.aset_department = d.department_id
          INNER JOIN a_item_list i ON r.item_id = i.item_id
          INNER JOIN a_notif_set s
          ON i.item_notif_set = s.notif_id AND s.notif_type = 'EMAIL'
    WHERE r.completed = 'N' AND (r.c_date IS NULL AND r.n_date IS NULL);


--
-- VW_OWNR_BY_USERS  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_DISTRICT (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--   A_THANA (Table)
--   A_USERS_AREA (Table)
--   A_USERS_BUS (Table)
--   A_USERS_COUNTRY (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_OWNR_BY_USERS
(OWNR_ID, OWNR_DEPT, OWNR_AREA, OWNR_TEXT, OWNR_USER, 
 OWNR_NAME, OWNR_DESG, OWNR_TYPE, OWNR_RESIGN, OWNR_POST, 
 ACT, UB_USER)
AS 
select a.ownr_id, a.ownr_dept, a.ownr_area, a.ownr_text, a.ownr_user,
a.ownr_name, a.ownr_desg, a.ownr_type, a.ownr_resign, a.ownr_post,
a.act,ub.ub_user
from a_ownr a join a_department d on a.ownr_dept = d.department_id
join a_users_bus ub
on d.department_business = ub.ub_business
and ub.act = 1
and ub.ub_user = ub.ub_user
where a.ownr_area in (
select f.area_id
from a_users_country c join a_district d
on c.country_id = d.district_country
join a_thana e on d.district_id = e.thana_district
join a_area f on e.thana_id = f.area_thana
where c.user_text = ub.ub_user and c.act = 1
union all
select a.ua_area
from a_users_area a
where ua_user = ub.ub_user and a.act = 1);


--
-- VW_RPT_ASET_EXP  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_BUSINESS (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_INSPH (Table)
--   A_INSP_TYPE (Table)
--   A_JOBH (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--   VW_RPT_JOBD_S1 (View)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_RPT_ASET_EXP
(JOBH_ID, JOBD_LINE, JOBD_ITEM, ITEM_NAME, JOBD_IQTY, 
 JOBD_IRATE, JOBD_IAMNT, JOBD_JQTY, JOBD_JRATE, JOBD_JAMNT, 
 JOBD_NOTE, INSPH_ID, JOBH_ASET, ASET_CODE_NAME, JOBH_OWNR, 
 OWNR_USER, JOBH_DEPARTMENT, DEPARTMENT_NAME, BUSINESS_ID, BUSINESS_NAME, 
 JOBH_AREA, AREA_NAME, JOBH_DATE, JOBH_MILEAGE, JOBH_VENDOR, 
 JOBH_NOTE, JOBH_TYPE, ITYPE_NAME, INSPH_IUSER, CDU)
AS 
SELECT JD.JOBH_ID,
           JD.JOBD_LINE,
           JD.JOBD_ITEM,
           JD.ITEM_NAME,
           JD.JOBD_IQTY,
           JD.JOBD_IRATE,
           JD.JOBD_IAMNT,
           JD.JOBD_JQTY,
           JD.JOBD_JRATE,
           JD.JOBD_JAMNT,
           JD.JOBD_NOTE,
           J.INSPH_ID,
           J.JOBH_ASET,
           A.ASET_CODE_NAME,
           J.JOBH_OWNR,
           O.OWNR_USER,
           J.JOBH_DEPARTMENT,
           D.DEPARTMENT_NAME,
           B.BUSINESS_ID,
           B.BUSINESS_NAME,
           J.JOBH_AREA,
           R.AREA_NAME,
           J.JOBH_DATE,
           J.JOBH_MILEAGE,
           J.JOBH_VENDOR,
           J.JOBH_NOTE,
           J.JOBH_TYPE,
           T.ITYPE_NAME,
           I.INSPH_IUSER,
           I.CDU
      FROM VW_RPT_JOBD_S1  JD
           INNER JOIN A_JOBH J ON JD.JOBH_ID = J.JOBH_ID
           INNER JOIN A_ASET_DATA A ON J.JOBH_ASET = A.ASET_ID
           INNER JOIN A_OWNR O ON J.JOBH_OWNR = O.OWNR_ID
           INNER JOIN A_DEPARTMENT D ON J.JOBH_DEPARTMENT = D.DEPARTMENT_ID
           INNER JOIN A_BUSINESS B ON D.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS = B.BUSINESS_ID
           INNER JOIN A_AREA R ON J.JOBH_AREA = R.AREA_ID
           INNER JOIN A_INSP_TYPE T ON J.JOBH_TYPE = T.ITYPE_ID
           INNER JOIN A_INSPH I ON I.INSPH_ID = J.INSPH_ID 
     WHERE J.JOBH_APP = 'Y';


--
-- VW_RPT_BILL  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_BILL (Table)
--   A_BUSINESS (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_INSP_TYPE (Table)
--   A_JOBH (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_RPT_BILL
(BILH_ID, BILH_DATE, BILH_NOTE, BILH_REF, BILH_RECEIVER, 
 BILH_IPAY, BILH_UPAY, BILH_CPAY, BILH_DISC, JOBH_ASET, 
 ASET_CODE_NAME, JOBH_OWNR, OWNR_USER, JOBH_DEPARTMENT, DEPARTMENT_NAME, 
 DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, BUSINESS_NAME, JOBH_AREA, AREA_NAME, JOBH_TYPE, 
 ITYPE_NAME)
AS 
SELECT   B.BILH_ID, B.BILH_DATE, B.BILH_NOTE, B.BILH_REF, B.BILH_RECEIVER,
            B.BILH_IPAY, B.BILH_UPAY, B.BILH_CPAY, B.BILH_DISC, J.JOBH_ASET,
            A.ASET_CODE_NAME, J.JOBH_OWNR, O.OWNR_USER, J.JOBH_DEPARTMENT,
            D.DEPARTMENT_NAME, D.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS, U.BUSINESS_NAME,
            J.JOBH_AREA, R.AREA_NAME, J.JOBH_TYPE, T.ITYPE_NAME
       FROM A_BILL B INNER JOIN A_JOBH J ON B.JOBH_ID = J.JOBH_ID
            INNER JOIN A_ASET_DATA A ON J.JOBH_ASET = A.ASET_ID
            INNER JOIN A_INSP_TYPE T ON J.JOBH_TYPE = T.ITYPE_ID
            INNER JOIN A_OWNR O ON J.JOBH_OWNR = O.OWNR_ID
            INNER JOIN A_AREA R ON J.JOBH_AREA = R.AREA_ID
            INNER JOIN A_DEPARTMENT D ON J.JOBH_DEPARTMENT = D.DEPARTMENT_ID
            INNER JOIN A_BUSINESS U ON D.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS = U.BUSINESS_ID
      WHERE B.ACT = 1 AND B.BILH_PAID = 'Y'
   ORDER BY A.ASET_ID;


--
-- VW_RPT_INSURANCE  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_BUSINESS (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_INSUD (Table)
--   A_INSUH (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_RPT_INSURANCE
(INSU_ASET, ASET_CODE_NAME, ASET_AREA, AREA_NAME, DEPARTMENT_ID, 
 DEPARTMENT_NAME, BUSINESS_ID, BUSINESS_NAME, INSU_ID, INSU_LINE, 
 INSU_ASET_VALUE, INSU_PREMIUM, INSU_VAT_AMOUNT, INSU_DATE, INSU_PAID, 
 DACT, INSU_POLICY, INSU_COMP, INSU_COMP_ADDRESS, INSU_NOTE, 
 INSU_REG_END, HACT)
AS 
SELECT
H.INSU_ASET,A.ASET_CODE_NAME,A.ASET_AREA,R.AREA_NAME,T.DEPARTMENT_ID,T.DEPARTMENT_NAME,B.BUSINESS_ID,B.BUSINESS_NAME,
D.INSU_ID,D.INSU_LINE,D.INSU_ASET_VALUE,D.INSU_PREMIUM,D.INSU_VAT_AMOUNT,D.INSU_DATE,D.INSU_PAID,D.ACT AS DACT,
H.INSU_POLICY,H.INSU_COMP,H.INSU_COMP_ADDRESS,H.INSU_NOTE,H.INSU_REG_END,H.ACT AS HACT
FROM A_INSUD D
INNER JOIN A_INSUH H ON D.INSU_ID=H.INSU_ID
INNER JOIN A_ASET_DATA A ON H.INSU_ASET=A.ASET_ID
INNER JOIN A_AREA R ON A.ASET_AREA=R.AREA_ID
INNER JOIN A_DEPARTMENT T ON A.ASET_DEPARTMENT=T.DEPARTMENT_ID
INNER JOIN A_BUSINESS B ON T.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS=B.BUSINESS_ID
ORDER BY D.INSU_LINE;


--
-- VW_RPT_POLICE_CASE  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_BUSINESS (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--   A_PLEA (Table)
--   A_PLEA_REASON (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_RPT_POLICE_CASE
(PLEA_ID, PLEA_REF, PLEA_DESCRIPTION, PLEA_LOCATION, "Case On", 
 "Paid By", PLEA_REASON_ID, REASON_NAME, PLEA_OWNR, OWNR_TEXT, 
 OWNR_USER, PLEA_ASET, ASET_TAG, ASET_CODE_NAME, PLEA_DEPT, 
 DEPARTMENT_NAME, BUSINESS_ID, BUSINESS_NAME, PLEA_AREA, AREA_NAME, 
 PLEA_DATE, PLEA_STATUS, "Status", PLEA_APP_DATE, "Approval", 
 PLEA_AMNT)
AS 
SELECT p.plea_id, p.plea_ref, p.plea_description, p.plea_location,
          CASE
             WHEN p.plea_dfnd = 'ASSET'
                THEN 'Plate Number'
             WHEN p.plea_dfnd = 'USER'
                THEN 'Iquma Number'
             ELSE 'Unknown'
          END "Case On",
          CASE
             WHEN p.plea_paid_by = 'COMPANY'
                THEN 'Compnay Pay'
             WHEN p.plea_paid_by = 'USER'
                THEN 'User Pay'
             ELSE 'Unknown'
          END "Paid By",
          p.plea_reason_id, r.reason_name, p.plea_ownr, o.ownr_text,
          o.ownr_user, p.plea_aset, x.aset_tag, x.aset_code_name, p.plea_dept,
          d.department_name, b.business_id, b.business_name, p.plea_area,
          a.area_name, p.plea_date, p.plea_status,
          CASE
             WHEN p.act = '1'
                THEN 'Active'
             ELSE 'In Active'
          END "Status", p.plea_app_date,
          CASE
             WHEN p.plea_app IS NOT NULL
                THEN 'Approved'
             ELSE 'Not Approved'
          END "Approval",
          p.plea_amnt
     FROM a_plea p INNER JOIN a_plea_reason r ON p.plea_reason_id =
                                                                   r.reason_id
          INNER JOIN a_ownr o ON p.plea_ownr = o.ownr_id
          INNER JOIN a_aset_data x ON p.plea_aset = x.aset_id
          INNER JOIN a_department d ON p.plea_dept = d.department_id
          INNER JOIN a_business b ON d.department_business = b.business_id
          INNER JOIN a_area a ON p.plea_area = a.area_id;


--
-- VW_USER_ASSET_ID_STRING  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_BUSINESS (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_DISTRICT (Table)
--   A_THANA (Table)
--   A_USERS_AREA (Table)
--   A_USERS_BUS (Table)
--   A_USERS_COUNTRY (Table)
--   A_USERS_GROUP (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_USER_ASSET_ID_STRING
(ASET_ID, UG_USER)
AS 
select
---with permission 06-Sep-2020
aset.ASET_ID,grop.ug_user
from a_aset_data aset
--join a_body bdy on aset.aset_body=bdy.body_id
--join a_brand bnd on aset.aset_brand=bnd.brand_id
--join a_fuel fl on aset.aset_fuel=fl.fuel_id
--join a_color clr on aset.aset_color=clr.color_id
--join a_group gp on aset.aset_group=gp.group_id
--join a_area ara on aset.aset_area=ara.area_id
join a_users_group grop on aset.aset_group =grop.ug_group and grop.act=1-- and grop.ug_user='53785'
join a_department dept on aset.aset_department=dept.department_id and dept.act=1
join a_business buss on dept.department_business =buss.business_id and buss.act=1
--join a_company comp on buss.business_company=comp.company_id
join a_users_bus ub on buss.business_id=ub.ub_business and ub.act=1 and ub.ub_user=grop.ug_user
where aset.aset_area in (
select f.area_id from a_users_country c 
join a_district d on c.country_id=d.district_country
join a_thana e on d.district_id=e.thana_district
join a_area f on e.thana_id=f.area_thana
where c.user_text=grop.ug_user and c.act=1
union all
select a.ua_area
from a_users_area a where ua_user=grop.ug_user and a.act=1
);


--
-- VW_USER_ASSET_LIST  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_BODY (Table)
--   A_BRAND (Table)
--   A_BUSINESS (Table)
--   A_COLOR (Table)
--   A_COMPANY (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_DISTRICT (Table)
--   A_FUEL (Table)
--   A_GROUP (Table)
--   A_THANA (Table)
--   A_USERS_AREA (Table)
--   A_USERS_BUS (Table)
--   A_USERS_COUNTRY (Table)
--   A_USERS_GROUP (Table)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_USER_ASSET_LIST
(ASET_ID, ASET_TAG, ASET_CODE_NAME, AREA_NAME, BODY_NAME, 
 BRAND_NAME, FUEL_NAME, COLOR_NAME, GROUP_NAME, ASET_MODEL, 
 ASET_PO_ENGINE, ASET_GRN_CHASIS, ASET_CAPACITY, ASET_PRICE, ASET_REG_DATE, 
 ASET_HEIGHT, ASET_WEIGHT, ASET_MFG_YEAR, ASET_IMAGE, ASET_NOTE, 
 ASET_USED, ACT, DEPARTMENT_NAME, BUSINESS_NAME, COMPANY_NAME, 
 UG_USER)
AS 
select
---with permission 06-Sep-2020
aset.aset_id,aset.aset_tag,aset.aset_code_name,ara.area_name,
bdy.body_name,bnd.brand_name,fl.fuel_name,clr.color_name,gp.group_name,
aset.aset_model,aset.aset_po_engine,aset.aset_grn_chasis,aset.aset_capacity,
aset.aset_price, aset.aset_reg_date,aset.aset_height, aset.aset_weight,aset.aset_mfg_year,
aset.aset_image,aset.aset_note,aset.aset_used,aset.act,dept.department_name,buss.business_name,comp.company_name,grop.ug_user
from a_aset_data aset
join a_body bdy on aset.aset_body=bdy.body_id
join a_brand bnd on aset.aset_brand=bnd.brand_id
join a_fuel fl on aset.aset_fuel=fl.fuel_id
join a_color clr on aset.aset_color=clr.color_id
join a_group gp on aset.aset_group=gp.group_id
join a_area ara on aset.aset_area=ara.area_id
join a_users_group grop on aset.aset_group =grop.ug_group and grop.act=1-- and grop.ug_user='53785'
join a_department dept on aset.aset_department=dept.department_id and dept.act=1
join a_business buss on dept.department_business =buss.business_id and buss.act=1
join a_company comp on buss.business_company=comp.company_id
join a_users_bus ub on buss.business_id=ub.ub_business and ub.act=1 and ub.ub_user=grop.ug_user
where aset.aset_area in (
select f.area_id from a_users_country c 
join a_district d on c.country_id=d.district_country
join a_thana e on d.district_id=e.thana_district
join a_area f on e.thana_id=f.area_thana
where c.user_text=grop.ug_user and c.act=1
union all
select a.ua_area
from a_users_area a where ua_user=grop.ug_user and a.act=1
);


--
-- A_ASET_ACCE  (Table) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ACCE_ITEMS (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--
--   Row Count: 3774
CREATE TABLE PM.A_ASET_ACCE
(
  ASET_ID       VARCHAR2(30 CHAR)               NOT NULL,
  ACCE_ID       VARCHAR2(30 CHAR)               NOT NULL,
  AAI_DESC      VARCHAR2(50 CHAR),
  AAI_QUANTITY  NUMBER(3)                       DEFAULT 1,
  AAI_RATE      NUMBER(10)                      DEFAULT 1,
  ACT           NUMBER(1)                       DEFAULT 1,
  CDT           DATE                            DEFAULT TO_DATE(SYSDATE),
  UDT           DATE                            DEFAULT TO_DATE(SYSDATE),
  CDU           VARCHAR2(10 CHAR),
  UDU           VARCHAR2(10 CHAR),
  VAR           NUMBER(10)                      DEFAULT 1
)
TABLESPACE DATA1
PCTUSED    0
PCTFREE    10
INITRANS   1
MAXTRANS   255
STORAGE    (
            INITIAL          64K
            NEXT             1M
            MINEXTENTS       1
            MAXEXTENTS       UNLIMITED
            PCTINCREASE      0
            BUFFER_POOL      DEFAULT
           );


--
-- PK_A_ASET_ACCE  (Index) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_ASET_ACCE (Table)
--
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX PM.PK_A_ASET_ACCE ON PM.A_ASET_ACCE
(ASET_ID, ACCE_ID)
TABLESPACE USERS
PCTFREE    10
INITRANS   2
MAXTRANS   255
STORAGE    (
            INITIAL          64K
            NEXT             1M
            MINEXTENTS       1
            MAXEXTENTS       UNLIMITED
            PCTINCREASE      0
            BUFFER_POOL      DEFAULT
           );

ALTER TABLE PM.A_ASET_ACCE ADD (
  CONSTRAINT PK_A_ASET_ACCE
  PRIMARY KEY
  (ASET_ID, ACCE_ID)
  USING INDEX PM.PK_A_ASET_ACCE
  ENABLE VALIDATE);



--
-- VW_ASSETUSERMANAGERVM  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_ASET_USER (Table)
--   A_BODY (Table)
--   A_BRAND (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--   A_USERS (Table)
--   VW_USER_ASSET_ID_STRING (View)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_ASSETUSERMANAGERVM
(AU_ID, ASET_CODE_NAME, OWNR_TEXT, OWNR_USER, DEPARTMENT_NAME, 
 AREA_NAME, AU_SRDATE, T_USER, AU_SADATE, T_APPROVER, 
 AU_SNOTE, AU_ERDATE, R_USER, AU_EADATE, R_APPROVER, 
 AU_ENOTE, UG_USER, AU_TRNSTYPE, BRAND_NAME, BODY_NAME, 
 ASET_MODEL)
AS 
SELECT au.au_id,
           a.aset_code_name,
           o.ownr_text,
           o.ownr_user,
           d.department_name,
           r.area_name,
           au.au_srdate,
           a1.user_name     t_user,
           au.au_sadate,
           a2.user_name     t_approver,
           au.au_snote,
           au.au_erdate,
           a3.user_name     r_user,
           au.au_eadate,
           a4.user_name     r_approver,
           au.au_enote,
           v.ug_user,
           au.au_trnstype,
           b.BRAND_NAME,
           bo.BODY_NAME,
           a.ASET_MODEL
      FROM a_aset_user  au
           JOIN a_area r ON au.au_area = r.area_id
           JOIN a_department d ON au.au_dept = d.department_id
           JOIN a_aset_data a ON au.au_aset = a.aset_id
           JOIN a_ownr o ON au.au_user = o.ownr_id
           JOIN a_brand b ON a.ASET_BRAND = b.BRAND_ID
           JOIN a_body bo ON a.ASET_BODY = bo.BODY_ID
           JOIN vw_user_asset_id_string v ON au.au_aset = v.aset_id
           LEFT JOIN a_users a1 ON au.au_sruser = a1.user_text
           LEFT JOIN a_users a2 ON au.au_sauser = a2.user_text
           LEFT JOIN a_users a3 ON au.au_eruser = a3.user_text
           LEFT JOIN a_users a4 ON au.au_eauser = a4.user_text
     WHERE au.act = 1
-- AND (   au.au_sauser IS NULL
--    OR (au.au_eruser IS NOT NULL AND au.au_eauser IS NULL)
-- );
;


--
-- VW_MAIL_BODY  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_EMAIL (Table)
--   VW_INSURANCE_S2 (View)
--   VW_KSA_IQUMA_S2 (View)
--   VW_NOTIF_MAIL (View)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_MAIL_BODY
(REMIND_ID, PH0, PH1, PH2, PH3, 
 PH4, PH5, PH6, PH7, MAIL_SUBJECT, 
 MAIL_BODY, UPDT, EMAIL_ID, KEEP_IN)
AS 
SELECT N.REMIND_ID, N.ASET_TAG AS PH0, N.ASET_CODE_NAME PH1, N.NOTE PH2,
          N.ITEM_NAME PH3, N.REMINDER_NOTE PH4, N.C_DATE PH5, N.N_DATE PH6,
          N.ELAPSED PH7, N.MAIL_SUBJECT, N.MAIL_BODY, N.UPDT, M.EMAIL_ID,
          M.KEEP_IN
     FROM VW_NOTIF_MAIL N INNER JOIN A_EMAIL M
          ON N.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS = M.MAIL_BUSINESS
        AND N.ASET_GROUP = M.MAIL_GROUP
        AND M.ACT = 1
        AND M.MAIL_TYPE = 'IQAMA'
    WHERE N.MAIL_SEND = 1
   UNION ALL
   SELECT Q.OWNR_ID, Q.OWNR_TEXT PH0, Q.OWNR_USER PH1, Q.OWNR_NOTE PH2,
          Q.OWNR_NAME PH3, Q.MESSAGES PH4, Q.AAJ_DATE PH5, Q.OWNR_NDATE PH6,
          Q.DAYS PH7, Q.MAIL_SUBJECT, Q.MAIL_BODY, Q.UPDT, M.EMAIL_ID,
          M.KEEP_IN
     FROM VW_KSA_IQUMA_S2 Q INNER JOIN A_EMAIL M
          ON Q.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS = M.MAIL_BUSINESS
        AND Q.GROUP_NAME = M.MAIL_GROUP
        AND M.ACT = 1
        AND M.MAIL_TYPE = 'IQAMA'
    WHERE Q.NEED_MAIL = 1UNION ALL
SELECT S.INSU_ID, S.ASET_NAME_CODE, S.INSURANCE, S.COMPANY, S.INSU_NOTE,
       S.MESSAGES, S.INSU_REG_START, S.INSU_REG_END, S.DAYS, S.MAIL_SUBJECT,
       S.MAIL_BODY, S.UPDT, M.EMAIL_ID, M.KEEP_IN
  FROM VW_INSURANCE_S2 S INNER JOIN A_EMAIL M
       ON S.DEPARTMENT_BUSINESS = M.MAIL_BUSINESS
     AND S.ASET_GROUP = M.MAIL_GROUP
     AND M.ACT = 1
     AND M.MAIL_TYPE = 'INSURANCE'
 WHERE S.NEED_MAIL = 1;


--
-- VW_RPT_ASET_USER  (View) 
--
--  Dependencies: 
--   A_AREA (Table)
--   A_ASET_DATA (Table)
--   A_ASET_USER (Table)
--   A_DEPARTMENT (Table)
--   A_OWNR (Table)
--   VW_USER_ASSET_ID_STRING (View)
--
CREATE OR REPLACE FORCE VIEW PM.VW_RPT_ASET_USER
(TAG, CODE_NAME, ASET_DEPT, ASET_AREA, USER_ID, 
 USER_NAME, WORKING_DEPT, WORKING_AREA, START_DATE, START_NOTE, 
 END_DATE, END_NOTE, STAUTS, ASET_GROUP, UG_USER)
AS 
SELECT   c.aset_tag tag, c.aset_code_name code_name,
            dd.department_name aset_dept, aa.area_name aset_area,
            o.ownr_name user_id, o.ownr_user user_name,
            d.department_name working_dept, a.area_name working_area,
            u.au_srdate start_date, u.au_snote start_note,
            u.au_erdate end_date, u.au_enote end_note,
            CASE
               WHEN u.act = 0
                  THEN 'CLOSED'
               ELSE 'RUNNING'
            END stauts, c.aset_group, vw.ug_user
       FROM a_aset_user u INNER JOIN a_area a ON u.au_area = a.area_id
            INNER JOIN a_department d ON u.au_dept = d.department_id
            INNER JOIN a_ownr o ON u.au_user = o.ownr_id
            INNER JOIN a_aset_data c ON u.au_aset = c.aset_id
                                   AND c.aset_id <> 0
            INNER JOIN a_area aa ON c.aset_area = aa.area_id
            INNER JOIN a_department dd ON c.aset_department = dd.department_id
            INNER JOIN vw_user_asset_id_string vw ON c.aset_id = vw.aset_id
   ORDER BY c.aset_code_name, u.au_srdate;


ALTER TABLE PM.A_ASET_USER ADD (
  CONSTRAINT FK_ASET_USR_AREA 
  FOREIGN KEY (AU_AREA) 
  REFERENCES PM.A_AREA (AREA_ID)
  ENABLE VALIDATE);

ALTER TABLE PM.A_ASET_USER ADD (
  CONSTRAINT FK_ASET_USR_ASET 
  FOREIGN KEY (AU_ASET) 
  REFERENCES PM.A_ASET_DATA (ASET_ID)
  ENABLE VALIDATE);

ALTER TABLE PM.A_ASET_USER ADD (
  CONSTRAINT FK_ASET_USR_DEPT 
  FOREIGN KEY (AU_DEPT) 
  REFERENCES PM.A_DEPARTMENT (DEPARTMENT_ID)
  ENABLE VALIDATE);

ALTER TABLE PM.A_ASET_USER ADD (
  CONSTRAINT FK_ASET_USR_OWNR 
  FOREIGN KEY (AU_USER) 
  REFERENCES PM.A_OWNR (OWNR_ID)
  ENABLE VALIDATE);


ALTER TABLE PM.A_ASET_USER_IMG ADD (
  CONSTRAINT FK_IMG_AU 
  FOREIGN KEY (IMG_AU) 
  REFERENCES PM.A_ASET_USER (AU_ID)
  ENABLE VALIDATE);


ALTER TABLE PM.A_ASET_USR_ACE ADD (
  CONSTRAINT FK_ASET_ACCE_UA 
  FOREIGN KEY (AUA_ACCE_ID)
